@AGENTS.md

# RENUABL — working notes for Claude

## Read first

- `docs/design/` holds the approved visual design (brand sheet, desktop + mobile customer screens, installer portal). **Match it.** It overrides any earlier styling in this repo.

- `docs/product-contract/` is the UX contract (master prompt + four surface specs). When anything is ambiguous, prefer simplicity and those principles over adding features.
- `docs/product-contract/LIVE_SOFTWARE.md` (8 Nov 2026, Martin): RENUABL Business is live software, not a store of records. Every screen answers what has been done, what is about to happen and what is needed from me; Sol gives notice with a countdown instead of asking permission; he shows his work; the screen stays calm. Build every new screen this way. The next build in that direction is planned in `docs/plans/sol-first-contact.md`.
- `docs/BUSINESS_MODEL.md`: the household, not the lead or the job, is the core entity. Solar is product 1 of a catalogue.

## Product rules to preserve

- **RENUABL is a network of two sides (3 Nov 2026, Martin).** **RENUABL Home** is the direct-to-consumer side for homeowners (system owners): `/` (the homeowner page), the guides, the quote flow and My RENUABL. **RENUABL Business** is the retailer and installer side (the design tool, CRM and Marketplace below), whose front door is **`/business`** (`SalesPage`; a business already signed in goes straight to `/design`; indexed, with the Meta Pixel and HubSpot tracking as an ad landing page; `/design` signed out still shows the same page). Each links to the other: the homeowner top bar and mobile menu say **For solar businesses** (to `/business`, in place of "Become a partner"), and the business page's footer says **For homeowners** (its top bar is a menu of the page's sections on the left, RENUABL alone in the middle with no "Business" beside it, and Start demo and Sign up on the right (17 Nov 2026, Martin), with no homeowner link since 17 Nov 2026, Martin). Ads land on their own pages (`/quote`, `/battery`, `/business`), never `/`. **Hidden until launch** (5 Nov 2026, Martin: not discoverable on renuabl.com.au; reached by typing **/design** or **/crm**): while `BUSINESS_LINK_SHOWN` (business-link.ts) is false, the homeowner site has no link to it, `/business` is noindex and out of the sitemap, robots.txt keeps crawlers out of `BUSINESS_PATHS` (/business, /design, /crm), and `/crm` redirects to `/design`; set it true at launch. **Launched, and the front page (19 Nov 2026, Martin: "switch www.renuabl.com.au/business with www.renuabl.com.au with the current renuabl.com.au homepage saved so that i can consider using it at a later stage")**: `/` is now RENUABL Business (`SalesPage`, the site JSON-LD, `siteJsonLd` describing the business platform), `/business` sends there for good with its query kept (next.config.ts `redirects`; `/business/demo` stays), and `BUSINESS_LINK_SHOWN` is true (indexed, in the sitemap as the front page, /design and /crm out of the robots block). **The homeowner homepage is kept at `/homeowners`** (`(consumer)/homeowners/page.tsx`, noindex, linked from nowhere; the business page's footer lost its For homeowners link); to bring it back, move that file back to `(consumer)/page.tsx`. The rest of RENUABL Home (`/quote`, `/battery`, `/learn`, the plan, My RENUABL) is unchanged, but its wordmark and home links still go to `/`, which is now the business page. **"Powered by RENUABL" on proposals (23 Nov 2026, Martin: yes, "but it cannot be competing with the company's logo")**: the customer's proposal page (`/p/<link>`, `CustomerProposal`) has `PoweredByRenuabl` (proposal-view.tsx) fixed in the bottom left corner, 10px, muted, on a faint pill, so a screenshot carries it while the business's logo heads the page; the chat and Call me keep the right; and the footer of every proposal page (the business's preview too) ends "Powered by RENUABL" in small muted type. Not in the PDF, the share picture, the configurator or the order page yet. Not seen in the browser (no proposal in the local database). Not decided yet: folding the older installation partner program (`/partners`, still reachable) into RENUABL Business.

- Healthy Home (the brand message since 1 Oct 2026, copy in `domain/healthy-home.ts`, tested): "A healthier home, powered by the sun." Two packages planned together: Renewable (solar, battery, EV charger, heat-pump hot water, blackout backup; induction cooking was removed: not offered, never recommended) and Healthy home (air purification, air-quality monitoring, water filtration, circadian lighting, fresh-air ventilation, leak detection). Healthy home products aren't offered yet (research phase since 30 Sep 2026): never price, quote, "add to plan" or promise to install them anywhere; the Home Health check gathers what customers want. The home page marks the Healthy home package card and the example plan's Healthy home section "Coming soon" (`comingSoon`, `COMING_SOON`); remove it when the products are offered. Wording describes what a product does, never a health outcome ("lighting designed for better sleep", not "helps you sleep"). The home page (`page.tsx`, `home-sections.tsx`) has no mascot illustration: hero (on phones it fills the first screen: eyebrow + the headline over three lines (`HOME_HERO.headlineLines`) centred in the space above, then the three compact steps leading straight into the address box, the last thing on screen, with room below it clear of the Ask Revo launcher; no paragraph under the headline; below the fold a "Learn with Revo" card (Revo's avatar, `HOME_HERO.learnLine`) and the Home Health link; the home page's mobile header has "Learn" beside "Log in") + "Your home plan — Example" card (the label above the address reads "Your home plan, priced in about 2 minutes."), "Two packages" (photos `public/brand/package-renewable.webp` and `package-healthy.webp`, supplied by the business, cropped to 1200×628), the WHOOP banner, "How it works", the Home Health teaser, guides; Revo is only the small floating "Ask Revo" launcher (`AskRenuabl` `variant="launcher"`). Before checkout the quote flow stays on energy; the reserve step has one optional, unticked "I'm interested in healthy home upgrades" box (`jobs.healthy_home_interest`, HubSpot note).
- WHOOP launch offer (`WHOOP_OFFER.name` "Launch offer"; was "first 50 customers", then "October offer"; `domain/whoop-offer.ts`, `server/whoop-repo.ts`, tested): no end date since 5 Oct 2026 (`WHOOP_OFFER.endsOn` null; it was 31 Oct): open while stocks last (`cap`, stated in the fine print and terms); setting `endsOn` to a date closes it after that day (Melbourne time, `whoopOfferEnded`) and every mention disappears. A free WHOOP One in Jet Black only (`WHOOP_OFFER.colour`, in the fine print, gift line and terms) + 12-month membership (value `WHOOP_OFFER.value`, VERIFY on launch day) for orders with a battery, one per order, at most `cap` (50). `/api/reserve` claims on the server inside a transaction with an advisory lock (`whoop_claims`; released claims don't count); the reservation result, email gift line ($0) and confirmation say so only when claimed. Every WHOOP mention in the browser (`whoop-promo.tsx`, `useWhoopOpen` → `/api/offers/whoop`, never the count) stays hidden until the server says claims are left, so all of it disappears at the cap. Status claimed → shipped → delivered (My RENUABL shows Reserved/Shipped/Delivered); staff mark shipped/delivered or release an unshipped claim on `/admin` (there's no order cancellation yet, so a cancelled order is released by hand). Terms at `/offer-terms` (drafted, review before relying on it; says WHOOP doesn't sponsor it). Confirm gift-with-purchase with compliance alongside Solar Victoria and the battery incentive.
- Home Health check (`domain/home-health.ts` questions + `healthPlan` rules, tested; `HomeHealthCheck` at `/home-health`): research for now, since we haven't chosen the healthy home products. 27 optional questions (the brief's 23 plus direct interest questions: `air-interest`, `water-interest`, `priority` (what they'd most like help with) and `budget`), one per screen, progress bar, "Skip" on every screen and "Skip for now". After reserving (flow state has the reservation) it's saved on the order straight away (`/api/home-health` checks reference + email); from the home page it ends with "Save your answers" (email, address, `ConsentBoxes`), linked to the customer's latest order by email. Results (`HEALTH_RESULTS_COPY`) are information only: up to 3 ideas + at least one free fix, "Staying 10+ years" lists the kinds of upgrades worth keeping in mind; no score, no buttons to add or quote anything, and a note that we don't offer these products yet and the answers help decide which to offer first. `/admin` shows "Home Health answers": tallies of `RESEARCH_QUESTIONS` across all checks (`interestSummary`, `allHealthAnswers`), never the allergies answer. The old quote requests (`health_quotes`) are no longer written. The allergies answer is sensitive: only answered after its own consent tick, stored only with consent, never in HubSpot, staff emails or tallies (`shareableAnswers`). The confirmation page's next step is the check; one reminder email two days after reserving (`sendHealthReminders` in the daily cron, `jobs.health_reminded_at`); My RENUABL shows "Complete your Home Health check" until done, then the saved ideas. The Meta Pixel never runs on `/home-health`.

- **RENUABL runs as its own standalone CRM, not on HubSpot (3 Nov 2026, Martin).** New work keeps leads, calls and notes in RENUABL's own database; nothing new is written to HubSpot. The homeowner side's existing HubSpot writes (reserve, follow-up, quote-lead, consult, briefs, partners) are still to be moved across.
- **RENUABL's own homeowner leads are deals in its own CRM (3 Nov 2026, Martin; `server/renuabl-crm.ts`)**: RENUABL is a business on RENUABL Business, the account whose owner is `RENUABL_CRM_EMAIL`. A `/quote` lead and every `/api/follow-up` lead (`takeHomeLead`, after the answer) becomes a customer (found by email, else mobile, never overwritten) and a deal at "lead" with a note of every line the lead page knew, consent record included. The HubSpot writes still run beside it until they're moved across.
- **The phone assistant on CRM deals (Vapi, 3 Nov 2026, Martin: customers are sold only by signing the proposal, so calls answer questions, deal with objections and guide them to sign; `domain/voice-agent.ts` tested, `server/voice-calls.ts`, `/api/voice/vapi`, `/api/proposal/[token]/call`, `CallMe` in call-me.tsx; `voice-calls-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`)**. Two kinds of call, each tied to a deal (`queueCall`; the number is always the one on the customer's record): **a new lead** (`mode: "lead"`, RENUABL's own leads from `/quote` or "Prefer a hand with this?" with a mobile, who ticked consent to phone contact): answer questions, meet worries, say a proposal follows once the team has designed the system, and book the 15-minute call (`book_call`: only a time the booking page offers, `readBooking`; staff emailed with the invite, the lead sent `consultBookedEmail`) or ask the team to call back; **a customer who asked** (`mode: "proposal"`, `asked`: **Call me** under the greeting on `/p/<link>`, shown by `callMeFor` only where the business has calls on): go through the proposal from the customer's own copy (`customerFacts`, never a cost), meet objections and, when they're ready, guide them to "I'd like to go ahead" on the page; an indicative proposal can't be signed, so it offers a call back; `send_proposal_link` texts the link (only when SMS is set up). Every call: `request_callback` (written on the deal, the business's notify addresses else owners emailed `callbackRequestEmail`, RENUABL's staff texted), `no_more_calls`, `endCall`, `transferCall` when `VOICE_TRANSFER_NUMBER` is set. **The rules it keeps** (`voiceSystemPrompt`): says it's the business's automated assistant, by the name the business gave it, and that the call is recorded (`firstMessage`); only the facts given; never changes, discounts or negotiates a price, promises a date, rebate or saving, or agrees anything on the phone; no pressure (no invented deadlines, going ahead suggested at most once); never criticises another business; never asks for card, bank or ID details; the usual worries met by `OBJECTIONS` (price, other quotes, waiting, battery worth, partner, roof, legitimacy, not interested). Calls start only weekdays 9am–7:30pm and Saturdays 9am–4:30pm Melbourne time, never Sundays or Victorian public holidays (`VOICE_HOURS`); outside them Vapi holds the call for the next window (`schedulePlan`, `whenWords` tells the customer when), so no cron. A new lead is tried up to `MAX_ATTEMPTS` (3), a customer who asked `MAX_ASKED_ATTEMPTS` (2); a voicemail or last miss to a RENUABL lead texts the `/book-a-call` link (`missedCallSms`). Booking on `/book-a-call` stops RENUABL's lead calls still to come (`stopVoiceCalls`). **Everything is on the deal's own record** (activity kind `call`, by the assistant's name; each entry leads with what the call came to, `CALL_HEADLINES`, the time in bold when one was booked, `callRecordLine`/`bookedLine`, read back on the timeline by `readCallLine` in `CallEntryText`, call-entry.tsx, older "Revo called (automated)" lines too) and in `voice_calls`/`voice_call_events` with transcripts on `/admin` ("Phone assistant's calls"); never HubSpot. The assistant is sent inline with each call (`assistantConfig`: Claude via Vapi, `VAPI_MODEL` default `VOICE_MODEL`, claude-haiku-5-5 since 22 Nov 2026 at Martin's word: quicker and far cheaper than Sonnet; VERIFY on practice calls that the rules hold, and set `VAPI_MODEL` to claude-sonnet-5 to go back; **each voice agent may choose its own** (22 Nov 2026, Martin: "build the per-agent model setting"): the agent's cog → How it speaks → **How it thinks**, Default / Quicker / More careful (`CALLER_BRAINS`, `BRAIN_MODELS` quick claude-haiku-5-5, careful claude-sonnet-5, `BRAIN_WORDS`, `cleanBrain`, `modelOfBrain` in caller.ts, tested in `caller-brain.test.ts`; `design_caller.brain`, `setCallerBrain`, PUT `/api/design/caller` `{brain, role}` who may change settings, `saveCallerBrain`, `BrainSettings` in coach-training.tsx), used on every call that agent makes or answers, its booking calls, test and training calls (`briefFor` → `model`, `startPracticeCall`); Default is the site's model. Not yet in Settings → Phone assistants; not seen in the browser or tried on a call: VERIFY a test call on each), the webhook address carrying our reference and our secret in the `x-renuabl-voice` header. **For now only RENUABL's own deals are called** (`voiceOnFor`); other businesses come later. Off unless `VOICE_CALLS_ENABLED=true` with `VAPI_API_KEY`, `VAPI_PHONE_NUMBER_ID`, `VAPI_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (20+ characters), `SITE_URL` and `RENUABL_CRM_EMAIL`; optional `VAPI_VOICE_PROVIDER`/`VAPI_VOICE_ID`, `VOICE_TRANSFER_NUMBER`. `/api/status` says which are set. **Today's Calls tab** (8 Nov 2026, Martin; `domain/design/call-log.ts` tested, `server/design-call-log-repo.ts`, `/api/design/calls`, `CallLogPage` in call-log.tsx), between Deals and Inbox: the calls Sol still has lined up, then every call on the deals this member may see over `CALL_LOG_DAYS` (60), Sol's (`voice_call_events` ended, with outcome, summary and transcript) and the team's (activity kind call by a member), by day, All / Sol's / Team's; says when calls aren't on for the business. Today's first tab is **Deals** with Sol's mark (`SolIcon`). **The assistant is the business's own (12 Nov 2026, Martin: there's no Revo on RENUABL Business; Sol is RENUABL's in-built AI, and each retailer personifies its caller)**: Settings → Business → **Phone assistant** (`CallerSettings` in settings-caller.tsx, `domain/design/caller.ts` tested, `server/design-caller-repo.ts`, table `design_caller`, `/api/design/caller` and `/photo`, `services/caller.ts` `useCaller`): a name (`cleanCallerName`: letters, spaces, apostrophes and hyphens, it's said out loud; Sol until named) with how a call opens (`callerIntro`), and a picture (`avatarImage`: checked by its own bytes, cut square, 320 px JPEG), shown beside its calls on the deal (`CallEntryBy`) and in Today's Calls tab (`CallerAvatar`); with no picture it is a sound wave on graphite, not an initial and not a face (13 Nov 2026, Martin), in one of six colours by its name (`callerTint`, `--caller-0`…`5`; unnamed keeps the orange), three thicker bars under 24 px. **Its voice** (12 Nov 2026, Martin: a picker, so the voice suits the name): `CALLER_VOICES` (Vapi's own voices as listed on docs.vapi.ai, none Australian, and ElevenLabs' Charlie, Australian; VERIFY each on a test call), or any Vapi or ElevenLabs voice by its ID (`cleanVoice`), kept as `design_caller.voice_provider`/`voice_id` and sent on every call ahead of `VAPI_VOICE_ID` (`briefFor` → `voice`); **Practice a call** (14 Nov 2026, Martin: the test rang, said one line and hung up, with no timer and no way to hang up; "I want to be able to actually talk to him"; `domain/design/practice-call.ts` tested, `server/design-practice-call.ts`, table `design_practice_calls`, the member's own, business id first): `/api/design/caller/test` (`prices`, 6 an hour) has the assistant of that role ring the member's own mobile from their profile with everything a real call has (its rules, voice, coaching notes, the wait-for-hello opening, no audio kept), playing them as a new lead (`practiceBrief`, `practiceBlock` after every rule: its tools aren't connected, so it says what it would do; if they step out of the role it answers as a colleague), up to `PRACTICE_SECONDS` (600); nothing is booked, sent or written on a deal. Its webhook reference starts `pc_` (`isPracticeRef`): the webhook keeps only what is said (`keepPracticeLive`) and answers any tool with "practice". It opens in the assistant's live call window (`PRACTICE_ID` as its id; `ringMeToHear` watches it, `/api/design/caller/test/live` GET, POST to type to it, DELETE to hang up), with the clock, the transcript, the box to type to it and **Hang up**, but no deal actions. The button is a phone in Talk to <name>'s header and **Practice a call** in Settings. VERIFY on a real call: that it rings, talks, and hangs up from the window. **Who calls** (12 Nov 2026, Martin: Call anywhere on a deal or Today's feed lets you choose the caller): `CallChoices` in call-choice.tsx, in the deal's Call menu (on the page body) and as `CallChooser` from a Today card's Call round, its new-arrival card and its Call move: you on your phone (tel:), you with the note-taker, or the business's phone assistant by its name and face (`askAssistantToCall` → `/api/design/jobs/[id]/call`, `publish`, in calling hours); while the assistant isn't set up (`Caller.named`: no `design_caller` row) an admin (`mayChange`: may change settings) is sent to Settings → Phone assistant and anyone else is told to ask one; set up but calls not on for the business, it is shown greyed with why. **Calls coming in** (12 Nov 2026, Martin: answer people who ring the number back): Vapi asks the webhook for an assistant (`assistant-request`, no ref; the number's Server URL in Vapi is `<site>/api/voice/vapi` with the `x-renuabl-voice` header set to `VAPI_WEBHOOK_SECRET`); `answerInbound` finds the caller on RENUABL's deals by their number (the open run of calls on it, a call lined up for them cancelled; else their latest deal not lost, `findCustomerByMobile`; else a new lead from the number, source "Rang our number"), keeps the call on that deal (`lead.inbound`) and answers with the business's assistant told they rang in (`CallBrief.inbound`: "thanks for calling", no voicemail, `MAX_INBOUND_SECONDS`), reading their proposal when one is out; never tried again after; another number, a withheld one or any failure hears `closedAssistant`'s short message. Only RENUABL's own number for now; each business's own number comes later. VERIFY before going live: the Deepgram `en-AU` model name, cancelling a scheduled call with DELETE, voicemail detection, and the privacy policy naming Vapi; legal advice on the call script. **Changing a call they asked for (19 Nov 2026, Martin: "allow the call request to be cancelled. also upon being clicked on allow it to call now - the customer should know they'll be speaking with an ai assistant")**: `CallMe` asks the server whether a call they asked for is still waiting as the page opens (GET `/api/proposal/[token]/call`, `askedCallOf`: asked, lined up or waiting to try again, never ringing or on, never one the business lined up), so "We'll call you Friday at 9am." is there after a reload, with "With <name>, our AI assistant · Change" under it. Pressing it opens a card: that <name> is an AI assistant, not a person, **Call me now** (PUT, `ringAskedCallNow` through `callNowInstead`: their own press, so it rings outside calling hours too, noted on the deal) and **Cancel the call** (DELETE, `cancelAskedCall`: closed on our side, Vapi's held call cancelled, noted on the deal as the customer's); once ringing it says "You'll be speaking with <name>, our AI assistant." The Call me button says "our AI assistant" too. Tested in voice-calls-db.test.ts; the routes tried locally with no call waiting; **the card not seen in a browser** (calls are off locally) and not tried on a real call: VERIFY one of each on the live proposal.
- **Calling from the computer (12 Nov 2026, Martin: Call couldn't ring from the computer, "like VoIP", through Twilio).** `domain/browser-call.ts` (tested), `server/twilio-voice.ts` (tested: settings, the access token, Twilio's signature), `server/browser-calls.ts` (table `design_browser_calls`, business id first), `services/computer-call.ts`, `components/design/computer-call.tsx` (`ComputerCallBar`, mounted once in `DesignShell`). **You, from this computer** is first in `CallChoices` (the deal's Call menu and Today's `CallChooser`) where it is on (`useComputerCallsOn` → `/api/design/voice`: an hour's token from Twilio for the member, made outgoing only through our TwiML app; identity a hash, `identityFor`). A press asks `/api/design/jobs/[id]/dial` for a one-off ticket (the number on the customer's record, never one from the browser; a deal the member may see), the browser connects with Twilio's Voice SDK (`@twilio/voice-sdk`, loaded only then) carrying only the ticket, and Twilio asks `/api/voice/twilio` (signature checked against `SITE_URL`, `fromTwilio`; the ticket used once by the member it was made for within `TICKET_SECONDS`), which dials the customer from `TWILIO_CALLER_ID` (so a call back reaches the phone assistant), plays them the notice that the call may be recorded (`/notice`, `recordingNotice`) and records both sides (`record-from-answer-dual`; `BROWSER_CALL_RECORD=false` turns it off). The bar at the foot of every page: ringing, on a call with the timer, Mute, Hang up, then how it ended. `/done` writes an unanswered call on the deal (`browserCallHeadline`); `/recording` (`maxDuration` 300) keeps the recording and, when `TWILIO_INTELLIGENCE_SERVICE_SID` is set, has Twilio transcribe it (Voice Intelligence v2; channel 1 is ours, VERIFY) and Sol write it up from both sides as the note-taker does (`callNotesPrompt` with `both`, one `call-notes` credit), else says why not (`NOT_WRITTEN`); the entry ends with `[recording:<id>]` (`recordingLine`), which the deal's timeline plays (`CallEntryText`, `/api/design/calls/browser/[id]/recording`, members who may see the deal) and Today's Calls tab leaves out. **Only RENUABL's own business for now** (`mayCallFromBrowser`: the number is RENUABL's). Needs `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID`, `TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN`, an API key (`TWILIO_API_KEY_SID`, `TWILIO_API_KEY_SECRET`, made in US1) and a TwiML App (`TWILIO_TWIML_APP_SID`, Voice URL `<site>/api/voice/twilio`, POST); `/api/status` says which (`browserCalls`). VERIFY on a first real call: the microphone prompt, the notice, the recording and the transcript's channels; legal advice on recording notice wording.
- **Handing a call to the phone assistant, and watching it live (12 Nov 2026, Martin: pressing Call should feel like assigning the call to Charlie, with his picture; while he calls, a window shows how long it has run and a live transcript).** `CallChoices` (call-choice.tsx) opens on **Hand it to <name>** with the assistant's face (`CallerAvatar` 44 px), then "Or call <name> yourself" (from this computer, on your phone, with the note-taker). Handing it over (`/api/design/jobs/[id]/call`; a call already lined up answers `ok` with `already`, so the window opens on it) opens `AssistantCallWindow` (assistant-call.tsx, mounted once in `DesignShell`, `services/assistant-call.ts` `watchAssistantCall`): bottom right of every page, the assistant's face (a pulse while ringing or on the call), where it stands (`livePhase`, `phaseWords`: will call, ringing, on the call, will try again, called), the call's clock, then what is said as bubbles (the assistant left, the customer right, a line still being said faded), what it came to once over (`CALL_HEADLINES`, the time booked), fold and close (the call carries on). `domain/live-call.ts` (tested): `assistantConfig` asks Vapi for `status-update` and `transcript` messages too; the webhook keeps them (`readLiveEvent`, `keepLive` in voice-calls.ts: status, answered and ended times on `voice_calls.live_*` for the attempt now on, each line said in full in `voice_call_lines` (up to `MAX_LINES`), the line still being said in `live_partial`); `GET /api/design/jobs/[id]/call/live` (`liveCallFor`, members who may see the deal; the latest call still lined up or on, else from the last six hours) is asked every 1.5 s while the window is open, 10 s once over or the tab is hidden. The transcript is Vapi's (Deepgram), by machine. VERIFY on a real call: Vapi sends partial and final transcript messages with `role` and `transcriptType` as read, and the webhook keeps up with them. **On the call, and typing to it** (12 Nov 2026, Martin: make it plain the call is under way; let me give the assistant an answer or have it mention something; the transcript got my words wrong): while on the call the window's header pulses in the signal orange, it is ringed orange and the clock is lit orange with a beating dot; a box at its foot (`TellBox`, shown while `canSteer`) sends a note to the assistant mid-call through Vapi's live call control (`monitorPlan.controlEnabled` on the assistant; the call's `monitor.controlUrl` read from `status-update`, only on Vapi's hosts, `isControlUrl`, kept on `voice_calls.live_control_url` and never sent to a browser; `POST /api/design/jobs/[id]/call/live`, `steerCall`, members who may publish and see the deal, 60 an hour): by default an `add-message` the customer can't hear, worked in in the assistant's own words under every rule it was given (`teamCommand`), or ticked **Have <name> say it word for word**, a `say`; each is kept on the call as the team's line (roles `team`, `team-say`) and shown in the window as "You told Charlie: …" (`teamLineWords`). An agent is one of three kinds for now, each made once (`AGENT_KINDS`, `AGENT_WORDS`): the **sales assistant** and **admin assistant** customers meet (the two phone assistants, `design_caller` by role) and the team's **chief of staff** (`design_colleague`). **A quiet dashed plus under the assistants on the rail** (for a member who may change settings) opens **Your agents**: each agent the business has with where it stands (`agentStanding`: Ready, "Ready to chat · needs its own number to be rung from a phone", "Needs a phone number") and Open (the sales assistant's chat, the chief of staff's window; the admin assistant's Settings), then **Add an agent**: what it should do (each kind saying who it talks to, what it does and **whether it needs a phone number**, before anything is made), then its name, a voice or Choose later, and **What it needs** (`agentNeeds`, from what is really set up: calls on, `VAPI_PUBLIC_KEY`, the team line, chat). The phone assistants share the business's customer number; **the chief of staff needs a new number of its own to be rung from a phone** (never the one customers ring), and the form says so in a sun-tinted box at its top (`needsNewNumber`). **Numbers are RENUABL's to buy and set up for now**: the box's tick (on to start) and the agent's row send **Ask RENUABL for a number** (POST: only who may change settings, only while that agent really is short of one, 6 an hour; RENUABL's staff emailed who asked and why, `numberRequestNote`, `tellStaff`; nothing is bought, nothing kept, never from a demo); the agent is made either way and works as far as it can. Making it goes through the same routes as Settings (`addAgent`: the caller route with its role, or the colleague route), so the name and voice rules hold. RENUABL's staff also see the settings behind a missing need by name (`NEED_SETTINGS`). **One business number, and the chief of staff is chat only (14 Nov 2026, Martin: "let's skip the ability to call the chief of staff at this stage. Let's keep it down to the one phone number. However, a user will need to buy this phone number as an additional charge on their plan")**: what is said above about the chief of staff's own number no longer holds. `COLLEAGUE_CALLS` (colleague-chat.ts) is false: its window is **Chat** and **Settings** (its name only), with no Call button, no On your phone, no PIN and no voice (the calling code and routes stay for when it comes back), its round on the rail carries a chat mark, and it needs no number (`AGENT_WORDS.colleague.usesNumber` false, "Ready to chat"). **A business has one phone number**: both phone assistants ring from it and it takes their calls back (`agentNeeds`: "Your business phone number", `AgentStatus` is now `callsOn`, `chat`, `made`); while the business hasn't got it, adding a phone agent shows **It needs your business phone number** with why one is enough, and its row reads "Needs your business phone number". **The number is an extra on the plan, and its price isn't set** (`NUMBER_MONTHLY_CENTS` null, `numberPriceWords`): until it is, no price is shown, nothing is charged, and **Ask for my business number** only emails RENUABL's staff to confirm the price first and set it up. Not yet: the price, the charge on the Stripe subscription, buying the number through Vapi or Twilio, and calls for any business but RENUABL's own (`voiceOnFor`), which a business's own number depends on. Seen in the browser with sample answers standing in for the server (the local server lacked the chief of staff's tables): the plus, the list, the kinds, the number box, adding one and the request. Not yet: buying a number in the app (through Vapi or Twilio, charged to the business), a number of each business's own, agents of other kinds or more than one of a kind, a record of numbers asked for, and the admin assistant's own chat.

over the $29, 250 texts and 60 minutes with Sol credits beyond in the add-ons note above).** `ADD_ON_MONTHLY_CENTS.voice` 4900; `domain/design/voice-usage.ts` (tested): `VOICE_FREE` 30 minutes and 30 texts each calendar month in Melbourne's time (`usageMonth`), beyond them `VOICE_PRICES` = `VOICE_COSTS` (**PLACEHOLDER**: 40c a minute, 9c a text, ex GST; read the real figures from Vapi's call log and Twilio's pricing and set them) were cost × (1 + `VOICE_MARGIN` 0.5) + GST until Martin set them by hand the same day: **50c a minute and 30c a text, GST inclusive** (`VOICE_PRICES`; `marginOn` says what each leaves over the costs: against the placeholder costs about 14% on a minute and 200% on a text). **Counted** in `design_voice_usage` (server/design-voice-usage.ts, business id first, once by reference): every voice-agent call's minutes rounded up from Vapi's end-of-call report (`durationSeconds` on `readVapiEvent`; calls out and in, test and training calls too), and every text sent from the business's number by the parts it goes as (`keepText` out, the walkthrough text). **The cap** (`businesses.voice_cap_cents`, `DEFAULT_CAP_CENTS` $100, `cleanCap` whole dollars to $5,000; $0 stops each at its free allowance): once the month's charges reach it, `overCap` refuses new agent calls (`queueCall` → `cap`, `CAP_REACHED`) and texts (the deal's text route, the walkthrough text, routines), until the 1st; calls coming in are still answered and count; practice and training calls aren't stopped. **Charged** by the daily cron (`/api/cron/offers`, `chargeLastMonth`): last month's use beyond the free allowance as one Stripe invoice item on the business's subscription with the GST rate (`usageInvoiceLine`), once a month (`design_voice_usage_charges`), so it lands on the next bill. RENUABL's own business and ones not billed are counted, never charged or capped. Seen in the browser on the local example business at 260, 96, 48 and 32 px and on the charcoal. **Don't take the brain out, or swap it for another of the four, without asking.** **The large live sun keeps its own white brain lines (21 Nov 2026, Martin: the etch tried on it that day was not liked, "reinstate the previous look and make it consistent everywhere")**: `SunBrain` in live-sun.tsx (fine white lines, soft-light, brighter while he talks) is drawn over the disc in every `LiveSun`, so the hero, the Ask Sol slide, the sticky bar and the launch slide match; `BigSol` no longer takes `brain`. **The small icon matches (21 Nov 2026, Martin: "make the small icons match too")**: `SolMark` draws the same white lines (`SunBrainLines` in sol-icon.tsx, the one drawing both use) at 60% in soft light over its disc; the etched brain (`BrainFolds`, `BrainEtch`) is gone, so what the note above says about the etch no longer holds. **Every Sol in the product is the homepage's live sun (21 Nov 2026, Martin: "they all need to be the same (in the product portal) as the sol on the homepage (the ball of energy)")**: `SolMark` and `SolBadge` now come from `sol-sun.tsx` and draw the same WebGL sun as `LiveSun` (shader in `sun-shader.ts`) with the white brain lines; a page can hold dozens of Sols and a browser allows about sixteen WebGL canvases, so one hidden WebGL canvas renders the sun (256 px, about 30 frames a second, only while one is on screen, one still frame with reduced motion) and each Sol copies its frame into a 2D canvas of its own. His round's disc is 80% of its box (a badge's 90%) with the glow spilling past it; `glow={false}` clips to the disc. The SVG drawings stay as `SolMarkFlat` and `SolBadgeFlat` for where WebGL can't run. The orbit and the badge's stars are gone with the SVG; `SolIcon` (the one-colour glyph) and `SolStars` are unchanged. Seen in headless Chrome on the local example business (Today, the floating bar, his panel) and the 16 px Sol on the business page. **The browser tab's icon is Sol too (21 Nov 2026, Martin: "update the favicon with our sol sun brain")**: `src/app/icon.svg` (his disc with its glow and the white brain lines, drawn thicker so they read at tab size) and `src/app/favicon.ico` (16, 32 and 48 px made from that SVG with sharp); it replaced Next's triangle. The phone home-screen icon matches (Martin: "yes to on phones"): `src/app/apple-icon.png`, 180 px, the same sun on the graphite of the floating bar (iOS fills a see-through icon with black); it replaced `apple-icon.tsx`, the R on forest green. **Bigger, and the brain plain to see (22 Nov 2026, Martin: "the favicon needs to be bigger and the brain lines more noticeable")**: `icon.svg` is the disc nearly edge to edge (a thin orange rim for the glow) with the brain in thick white lines on a darker orange edge, filling most of the disc; the 16 px size in `favicon.ico` carries only the brain's outline and its split (the folds blur at that size), 32 and 48 px the whole brain; `apple-icon.png` is made from the same SVG.

In his drawer and on his page alike. **Key points** (a line Sol starts "! ", `SolText` in sol-text.tsx): a card of its own on the warm sun with an orange edge, a small orange round with the bulb and **Key point** in small capitals, its words larger and semibold. **Type** (`solRuns`, `namedItem` in sol-format.ts, tested: nothing is reworded): figures with their unit and dollar amounts (600 mm, 13.5 kWh, $1,200, 90%) and words he marked with ** are semibold and never broken across a line; a phone number is a tel: link and an email a mailto: link, underlined in orange; the first paragraph of a longer answer reads a touch larger (the answer first); a line ending in a colon before a list is its small heading; list dots are orange, and an item that opens "Name: …" has its name in bold. `SolText` is shared, so Today's chat and a deal's Sol tab get the type and the key card too (not the ground). Each row is read by `solRow` (tested): who or what it is about in medium weight, an amount it leads with set at the right in the figures' face ("Taylor Test, $18,400: …"), the rest quieter under it; an item with no short opening before a colon is one line. **A row's arrow takes that one up with Sol** (`OnPick`, passed only by `SolChat`: his drawer and page): it sends "Let's work on this one: <the row>" as the member's next message, credits as any answer; the deal's Sol tab and Today's chat draw the sections without the arrow. Blocks are a pad's room apart (`space-y-6`). **Mini threads** (`threadDepths`, `THREAD_DEPTH_MAX` 3 in sol-format.ts, tested; `Follows`, `THREAD_STEP` in sol-text.tsx; `.sol-follows`): a paragraph or plain list that follows on from the block before is stepped in 1.5rem a step and joined to it by a fine elbow; by rule, no AI: a paragraph follows on unless it opens with a word that turns to a new subject (Last, Next, Separately, Also, Finally…), and a section, key point, question or choice stands alone and ends the thread. `SolText` takes `threads` (only `SolChat` passes it). **The key point is the only pad** and is not italic: an orange tint with a fine orange edge, its words at weight 520 (`.sol-key`; the serif and `.sol-key-words` are gone, as is `.sol-pad`). Always on; the automated texts (missed call, booking link) are unchanged. **Replies**: Twilio calls `/api/voice/twilio/sms` when a text comes in to the number texts go from (**a person must set the number's "A message comes in" address in Twilio to `<site>/api/voice/twilio/sms`, POST**); only a request Twilio signed is taken (`signedByTwilio` over the address built from `SITE_URL`, with `TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN`), nothing is texted back, and `receiveText` (after the answer) finds the customer by the mobile on their record in RENUABL's own business (the only one that texts for now) and their deal (the newest not lost, else the newest), keeps the text, and **says it in that deal's team chat as Sol's note** (`solPostsOnDeal`: "Text from 0412 345 678: …", which also writes it on the deal's record) with whoever looks after the deal following that chat (`tellDealFollowers`: its owner, else the business's owners), so it shows under **Deals** in the team chat window with its unread count and adds to the count on the chat round. **STOP** as the whole reply (`stopWord`: stop, unsubscribe, cancel, end, quit, opt out) sets `no_texts_at`: the text route refuses that customer (409) and the box says so; START clears it. A text from a number on no customer's record is kept with no deal and listed at the top of the assistant's contact search (**Texts from numbers not on a customer**, 30 days, `strayTexts`). **The Text box** (`TextBox` in coach-contacts.tsx) shows the texts with that customer so far, theirs left and the business's right, and looks for a reply every 15 s while open. **The chat round flashes** (`.team-flash` in globals.css, on `TeamChatButton` while anything is unread and the window is shut): it fills with the signal orange and back every 1.6 s; lit and still with reduced motion. - **Training a voice agent, locked rules, and how it speaks (16 Nov 2026, Martin: "a 'live training' with the voice agent, where he entertains an open discussion with me and understands I'm not a customer but I'm his trainer"; for every business; "allow requesting a call from an agent to be training mode and test mode (calling a lead)"; "a method to lock things into his rules. This is critical so that it's repeated"; "program the voice agent so it doesn't sound so scripted. It pauses, breathes, doesn't ramble off an opening, treats it like an actual phone call").** `domain/design/training-call.ts`, `delivery.ts` (both tested; `locked-rules.test.ts` covers the rules and the calls), `server/design-practice-call.ts`, `/api/design/caller/train`, `components/design/coach-training.tsx`. **Ring me** (`RingMe`): the phone round in **Talk to <name>**'s header, and a row on the agent's day, offers the two calls the agent makes to the member's own mobile: **Train <name>** and **Test call** (the practice call, renamed; Settings → Phone assistants has both buttons). Both need `prices` and calls on for the business. **A training call** is a practice call of `kind` training (`design_practice_calls.kind`; `/api/design/caller/test?kind=training`): everything a real call has, then `trainingBlock` after every rule: it is talking to its trainer, not a customer; it opens as a colleague, talks openly, rehearses a moment when asked and steps back out, repeats instructions back, and refuses anything the fixed rules forbid. **Two ways the call reaches the script.** (1) **Locked in on the call**: the trainer says "lock that in", the agent says the rule back and asks "Lock that in?", and only on a yes calls its one tool (`TRAINING_TOOL` lock_in; the webhook's `pc_` branch → `lockInFromTraining`: a training call still on, at most `MAX_LOCKS_A_CALL` 8, cleaned as any note), which keeps it as a locked rule there and then (`design_practice_calls.locked`). (2) **Everything else waits**: when the call ends (Vapi's ended status, or the window asking) `writeUpTraining` claims the call once and has Claude read the transcript (`trainingPrompt`, `TRAINING_SCHEMA`; one deep Sol credit, reason training; only what the trainer plainly asked, never a note that breaks a fixed rule, nothing already locked) into proposals (`cleanTrainingAnswer`, up to `MAX_PROPOSALS`), kept on the call (`outcome`). **The card** (`TrainingCard`, above the chat, for `TRAINING_SHOWN_DAYS` 7): on the call now, writing it up, then what was locked on the call, the agent's summary, and each proposal with **Lock in**, **Keep as a note** or **Drop** (`answerTraining`, `keepProposal`; a note a person changed since the call is left alone; Lock them all in; Try again when the write-up failed or credits ran out; What was said shows the transcript). **Locked rules** (`CoachNote.locked`, `MAX_LOCKED` 15): given to every call under their own heading ahead of the notes (`coachingBlock(notes, business, locked)`: followed on every call, every time, as written; only the rules every call keeps come before them), never trimmed for room (`trimNotes`), and the chat can't reword or remove one (`applyCoaching`). Locked four ways: by voice on a training call, **Lock in** on a proposal, the padlock on a line in the script (`NoteRow`, PUT `{id, locked}`, `setLocked`), or asked in the chat ("lock that in": `lock` in `COACH_SCHEMA`, shown as Locked in under the answer). The script panel lists **Locked rules** above **Notes**. Every call reads the script through `scriptFor` (design-caller-repo.ts: notes, locked rules, delivery). **How it speaks** (`Delivery`, `design_caller.delivery`, the cog → How it speaks, `DeliverySettings`; PUT `{delivery}`): manner (plain or natural), pace, opening (word for word, or easy: who it is, a beat, then why), how long before it answers and the room behind it; **Make it a natural phone call** sets `NATURAL_DELIVERY`. Natural adds `deliveryBlock` after the rules (short turns, small acknowledgements, commas and the odd ellipsis for breath, stops when interrupted) and the easy opening changes `openingBlock` to two short beats in its own words, **always still its name, whose assistant it is and that it's automated**. Two Vapi settings follow it, sent only when not Vapi's defaults (`deliveryParams`: `startSpeakingPlan.waitSeconds` 0.8 or 1.2, `backgroundSound` off; field names read from docs.vapi.ai 16 Nov 2026). **A sales agent nobody has set speaks naturally (17 Nov 2026, Martin: "make natural delivery the default for Charlie")**: `deliveryOf` answers what was chosen, else `defaultDelivery` (natural for every sales agent, plain for the post-sale one), so every sales agent's calls changed on that day without a press; a choice is kept as chosen (plain too), and **Make it plain again** sets plain. **Out of call credit (17 Nov 2026, Martin: "Charlie isn't calling me"; Vapi had refused with "Your Wallet Balance is -0.07" and the window only said to try again)**: a train, test or deal call Vapi turns away for want of credit says so (`outOfCallCredit`, `OUT_OF_CALL_CREDIT` in `domain/call-credit.ts`, tested; the practice call's result and `queueCall`'s `failed` carry `credit`, read from the refusal or the call's `stop_reason`); nobody is emailed about it yet, and the voice test in Settings and Call now still say only that the call couldn't be placed. When calls fail, look for "not placed" in Vercel's logs first. **Answering sooner (17 Nov 2026, Martin: "he's waiting 2 to 3 seconds before replying which creates awkward silences. He needs to reply within 1.2 seconds")**: the silence was mostly ours and Vapi's defaults: the natural default's 0.8 s pause on top of Vapi waiting 1.5 s on a sentence its transcriber gave no punctuation. Now every call carries `startSpeakingPlan` whole (`deliveryParams`, `END_OF_TURN` in delivery.ts, tested): the pause is 0.2 s for quick, 0.5 and 1.0 for the others (`PAUSE_SECONDS`; the natural default is quick now), Vapi's English end-of-turn model reads whether they have finished (`smartEndpointingPlan` livekit, waiting from next to nothing up to 0.9 s), and the no-punctuation wait is 0.8 s; an ElevenLabs voice is spoken by Flash v2.5 (`fastVoice` in voice-agent.ts). If Vapi answers 400 the call goes once more without those (`placeVapiCall` in server/vapi-call.ts, `withoutSpeedSettings`), so a refused setting slows calls and doesn't stop them. Field names read from docs.vapi.ai 17 Nov 2026; **not tried on a real call**: VERIFY the reply time (Vapi's call log gives each turn's `turnLatencies`), that it doesn't talk over a slow speaker, and how Flash sounds. What is left is the model's time to its first word: since 22 Nov 2026 the default is Haiku (`claude-haiku-5-5`, `VOICE_MODEL`), Martin's call; a `VAPI_MODEL` set in Vercel still wins. **Hearing voices without a call, and more of them (18 Nov 2026, Martin: "is there a way i can trial hearing more agent voices?")**: each voice in the picker (`VoicePicker`, Settings and the agent's cog) has a play round that plays its maker's own sample clip in the browser (`voiceSample`, `voiceSampleUrl` in caller.ts, tested; `server/voice-samples.ts`, `GET /api/design/caller/voice-sample?provider=&id=`, signed-in members, fetched by the server and kept a day in memory): Deepgram's from its fixed address, ElevenLabs' ready-made voices' from its public list (no key; only its own hosts, `isElevenLabsClip`); Vapi publishes none, so its voices show a muted mark and `NO_SAMPLE`; a voice typed by its ID gets the round too (an ElevenLabs library voice has no clip without a key). The clip is not the agent's words. **Six more voices**: provider `deepgram` joins vapi and 11labs (`voiceProvider`; sent with `model: "aura-2"` by `fastVoice`, kept on the 400 retry): Deepgram's Australian Hyperion and Theia, and ElevenLabs' British George, Daniel, Alice and Lily. ElevenLabs' only ready-made Australian voice is Charlie; more Australian voices are in its library, which Vapi reaches only with an ElevenLabs key added in Vapi's Integrations (then paste the voice's ID). Seen in the browser on the local example business (seven play rounds, clips served, play and stop); **none of the six tried on a call**: VERIFY each, Deepgram's first. Not yet: Azure's Australian voices (no sample to play), the play round in Your agents' add form. A voice can't breathe: what reads as breath is short turns and pauses, and the copy doesn't promise more. Seen in the browser on the local example business with a sample training call put in by hand (the write-up against Claude: three right proposals, the 10% discount refused, the locked rule not repeated; Lock in, Keep and Drop; the padlock; the natural preset; lock that in asked in the chat). **Not tried on a real call** (calls are off locally): VERIFY a training call end to end (that it opens as a colleague, the read-back, the lock_in tool reaching the webhook, the write-up arriving), a natural-delivery call (that Vapi accepts `startSpeakingPlan`, how the easy opening sounds, that it still says it's automated), and that a locked rule is followed on a customer call. Not yet: training from the computer's microphone (needs `VAPI_PUBLIC_KEY`), a choice of who the member plays on a test call, a list of earlier training calls, the voice's own speed, and the allowances counted (training minutes aren't metered).

The agent's picture at the top of **Talk to <name>** (`AvatarEdit` in coach-chat.tsx) no longer opens the file picker: it opens a small card with **Colour** (the six `--caller-N` colours as rounds, the one in use ringed, and Back to the colour of its name once one is chosen), then **Upload a picture** (or Change the picture and Remove the picture when it has one). A colour is the sound wave's, so choosing one puts any picture away first. `CallerAvatar` draws `tintOf(caller)` everywhere the agent shows (the bar, its day, calls, deals). Each agent's colour is its own. Seen in the browser on the local example business (the card, Pink chosen and shown on the bar, the day and a deal's call). Not yet: the same card in Settings → Phone assistants (it still has only the picture upload), and colours beyond the six.
- **The centre menu in liquid glass (19 Nov 2026, Martin: "it looks very flat. i want it to liven up a little"; chosen from three finishes in `tools/demo/nav-glass-preview.html`)**: the sections pill (`studio-sections` in design-shell.tsx) is `.glass-bar` (globals.css): see-through white over a blur of the page, a sheen across its top half (`::before`), a rim of light all round and a deeper lift; the lit section is `.glass-lit` (a dark glass bead), each word `.glass-item` catches the light on hover, the designer's pen is `.glass-orb` (a lit orange orb, like Sol's sun at the other end) and the proposals round `.glass-bead`. The styles were seen applied in the browser; the look not judged at full size there. **The rest in glass too** (19 Nov 2026, Martin: "do the same to the other menus like the floating one on the right and the one in the top right corner. you could do it also to the table headers of the feed and deals"): the floating bar (`SolRail` in sol-dock.tsx) and the top right card (`data-tour="icons"` in design-shell.tsx) are `.glass-bar` (it no longer sets its own position, so the fixed bar stays fixed; its sheen is `min(42%, 1.5rem)` tall with the element's own corners); Today's strip and the Deals strip (`STRIP_BOX` in deal-feed.tsx) are `.glass-strip`, dark glass: graphite in a gradient, a light top edge, a fine rim and a soft glow from the top. **`.glass-dark` is another thing** (the designer's controls on the roof picture, which turns the tokens dark): don't put it on a strip, or white buttons in it lose their ink. Seen in the browser at phone and desktop width.
- **One sample deal, and the tour of a deal (21 Nov 2026, Martin: "a new user shouldnt open up the pipeline to see a bunch of sample cards. Maybe just one - that when clicked on starts a tour of how to navigate a deal card. explains all the featuers on it").** `SAMPLE_CUSTOMERS` (crm.ts) is one: Sam Sample, a lead for solar and a battery, no place on the map. Businesses made before keep the samples they were given. On the pipeline its card carries a pill, **Sample deal · open it for a tour** (`SAMPLE_DEAL_HINT`; `listJobs` answers `sample`, true only for the starter sample, never the example deals a demo is filled with, source `Example deal`), and opens the deal with `?tour=1`. **The tour** (`DEAL_TOUR` in `domain/design/deal-tour.ts`, tested; run by `Spotlight` in new-look.tsx from `DealPage`): ten steps, each lighting its place (`data-tour="deal-…"`): the banner, Sol's card, Design, Proposal, Call Email and Files, Details, the four views inside it, the fields, the menu growing with the deal (job card, Service, Sol), and Deals with how to delete the sample and add a real deal. It runs every time the sample is opened from the pipeline, the first time on a device when opened any other way (`DEAL_TOUR_KEY`), and from **Sample deal · Show me around** beside Deals on its page; Escape or a press outside ends it. The spotlight lights the copy of a place that is on screen (`shownAt`: the deal's menu is drawn twice, a rail and a row). Seen in the browser on the local example business at 1500px (all ten steps); not seen on a phone, and not on a brand-new account (the local business still has its old five). Not yet: the pipeline's other cards in the tour, the tour on a phone's row menu checked, and the sample given a place on the map so Design can be tried.
- **Deals on a phone (22 Nov 2026, Martin, with a mock-up for reference: the Deals page on mobile was "suboptimal").** `PipelinesPage` (design-sales.tsx) under 640px: one graphite card heads the page (the pipeline as its title, pressed to pick another, with how many deals and their worth under it, New deal beside it; under them whose deals and Cards | List as two icons); the stages scroll sideways, one column at 86% of the width so the next peeks, snapping to each (`snap-x`); each column has a fine bar on top (`stageBar`: orange while being sold, green once won, red when lost) and its count and total ("$22k", `shortDollars`). Each card, at every width: the customer and the price on one line, the street and what it is, the deal's heat and clock, then the owner, how long since it changed (`agoWords`: Today, 2d ago, 3w ago) or the sample's tour pill, and Design it / Open design. **The stage dropdown on each card is desktop only**: on a phone a deal's stage is changed on the deal itself (drag doesn't work by touch). Making, renaming or removing a pipeline, and adding, renaming or moving its stages, are desktop only too (the bar on a column of the business's own stage follows the stage it counts as, `baseAt`). Seen in headless Chrome at 390px and 1440px on the sample retailer with example deals; not on a real phone.
- **More deals in view on the pipeline (22 Nov 2026, Martin: "fit a little more deal cards into view by being more efficient with whats in them"; over the note above where they differ).** In `PipelinesPage` (design-sales.tsx), at every width: **the board scrolls inside itself** down to the foot of the window (its top measured on load and resize, `--board-top`; 6rem kept clear for the phone's foot bar), so **each column's head stays put** as its cards scroll under it (`sticky top-0` inside the board; the page can't hold them, since a board that scrolls sideways can't have a head stuck to the window). **No New deal button**: the Lead column's head has **+ Add new deal** (a pipeline with no lead stage keeps the button up top). A card is the customer and price, the street with **icons for what it is** (`KitMarks`: the deal banner's panel, battery and charger, by `jobHas`; a deal that is none of them keeps its name in words, and the words are the icons' hover title), the heat and clock with how long since it changed at its right (`PulseMarks` `end`), then one row of the owner, the stage (computer only) and **the designer as an orange round with no words** (Open design or Design it as its hover title). On a phone a deal with no place on the map has no last row (its owner sits by the time). The stage dropdown on a card is small (22 Nov 2026, Martin: "make the stage dropdown smaller too": 24 px tall, 11px words). A desktop card went from about 160 px to about 120. Seen in headless Chrome at 1440px and 390px on the sample retailer's example deals (none on the map, so the designer round not seen).
- **The pipeline on a phone: one way at a time, with momentum (22 Nov 2026, Martin, from his iPhone: "I shouldn't be able to pull the deal cards around the page. The top headers should be fixed and locked and then the page only slides across with momentum… the harder I swipe the faster it moves and then gravity slows it down. Same for scrolling down").** Under 640px in `PipelinesPage` (design-sales.tsx) the board scrolls sideways only (`overflow-x-auto overflow-y-hidden overscroll-x-contain`), its height the screen less the board's top and `8.5rem` (the pad's foot and the room for the foot bar), so the page itself never scrolls and the pipeline card stays put; each column scrolls its own cards (`ul` `overflow-y-auto overscroll-y-contain`) under its head, which no longer moves; the stops between columns are soft (`snap-proximity`, was `snap-mandatory`), so a flick carries on with the phone's own momentum and settles on a column when it comes to rest near one. **Every phone page lost about 76px of scroll that was nothing**: the frame kept two bottom paddings for the foot bar (`pb-24` on the frame and `pb-20` inside it, now `pb-4 lg:pb-20`) and the pad's least height is `100dvh-15rem` under 640px (was 10rem). Larger screens unchanged. Seen in headless Chrome at 390 × 760 on the local example business (the page 760 tall in a 760 window, the board sideways only, a column scrolling); not on a real iPhone: VERIFY the swipe feel and that no other phone page now ends short of its foot.
- **No Deals column under Today's feed on a phone (22 Nov 2026, Martin: "the deals section is all the way at the bottom. This doesn't work… removed from here for mobile").** `DealsPanel` in deal-feed.tsx is `max-lg:hidden`: under 1024px (phones and tablets, the widths where it fell under the feed) Today's feed has no Deals list at its foot (the foot bar's Deals tab, or Deals in the menu, opens the pipeline); from 1024px it sits beside the feed as before (Martin, the same day: "hide it on tablets too"). Not seen on a phone or tablet.
- **The wordmark stays in every phone screenshot (23 Nov 2026, Martin: the RENUABL logo scrolls out of view, which is right for room on screen, but "if someone screenshots and shares, I want the recipient to see the logo"; on most screens where sensible).** `PhoneTop` (phone-top.tsx, in `DesignShell` for every page in the frame): the phone's top row (the wordmark, or ← Deals on a deal, and Search) as before; once it has scrolled out of view (an `IntersectionObserver` on the row) a slim bar fades in fixed at the top of the screen (`.phone-mini-top`: 36 px under the safe area, the canvas at 85% with a blur and a hairline under it): RENUABL small (11px, wide tracking) in its middle, pressed to go to Today, the way back on its left on a deal, and Search on its right; it goes again as the row comes back. Phones only (under 1024px; the desktop's top bar never scrolls away). A page that scrolls inside itself (the pipeline board) never hides the row, so needs no bar. Seen in headless Chrome as an iPhone 13 on the local example business (Customers scrolled); not on a real iPhone. Not yet: the same on the customer's proposal and the homeowner pages.
- **The settings cog (13 Nov 2026, Martin: the profile round's initials, "MA", meant nothing)**: the round at the top right (`ProfileMenu` in shell-menus.tsx) shows a cog; its menu is unchanged (who is signed in, Settings, Your account, Industry news, Updates, Feedback, Sign out).
- RENUABL recommends one system, sized from the customer's electricity bill. No catalogues.
- The customer uploads their latest bill on "About your home"; Claude reads it server-side (`src/app/api/bill/route.ts`, `src/lib/server/bill-reader.ts`) and `src/lib/domain/bill.ts` validates the figures. Ask only for usage and prices, never personal details, and don't store the file. If Claude is overloaded (429/5xx), `readWithClaude` retries once, then tries `ANTHROPIC_FALLBACK_MODEL` (default `claude-sonnet-5`). On phones the main option is "Take a photo" (opens the camera), with "Upload a file" for PDFs and screenshots; desktop shows "Upload bill". The bill is the best way in, not the only one (trial from 4 Oct 2026, 7 days, because the upload looked like the drop-off point for Meta ad visitors; `SPEND_ESTIMATE_ENABLED` in `domain/spend-estimate.ts`, tested): the upload carries a "Most accurate" badge, then "or" and "Tell us roughly what you spend" (`SpendEstimate`): billing period (Monthly / Every 2 months / Quarterly) and four spend bands (`SPEND_BANDS`, a month: up to $150, $150–$300, $300–$450, over $450, scaled for the period) → `billFromSpend` (spend less a PLACEHOLDER daily supply charge, divided by a PLACEHOLDER usage rate, `SPEND_ASSUMPTIONS`) gives a stand-in `BillSummary` with `estimate` set. Everything built from it says "Indicative" (`isIndicative`, `SPEND_COPY`): the savings reveal, the system step's subtitle, savings card and "How we worked this out", Revo's lines, the reserve step's price (`priceNote`), the order email (`INDICATIVE_PRICE_NOTE`) and the HubSpot "Bill usage" line (INDICATIVE + the spend range, "get the bill on the call"). Never call it as accurate as a bill; no typed-in kWh. A real bill read later replaces it. Meta gets the custom `SpendEstimated` once (`spendTracked`), never the amount. Continue stays pressable without either: pressing it scrolls to the upload with "Please upload your latest bill, or tell us roughly what you spend, to continue". "Don't have your bill handy right now?" (`BillLater`, `/api/follow-up`, sage card) sits at the bottom of the page, just above Continue, whenever there's no bill: it takes an email, a mobile or both (at least one, `readFollowUpContact`): HubSpot contact (by email, else by mobile) + "Finish later: no bill yet" note (home, ad source), staff alert, and a `finishLaterEmail` link back when there's an email (it also offers "Book a 15-minute call" → `/book-a-call`: `BookACall`, Monday–Saturday times from `buildCallAvailability`, name + mobile + email + consent, `/api/consult` checks the slot (`isBookableCallSlot`), builds the label, HubSpot contact + note, staff alert (`kind: "call"`) and `consultBookedEmail` with an invite (`consultEvent`); HubSpot Meetings is embedded instead when configured; a chat with the team, no obligation, not the post-reservation confirmation call); hidden once a bill is read. The bill must be for the home entered: the address is sent with the bill (`addressForBillCheck`), Claude answers only yes / no / unclear for the supply address (never returns it), and only a clear "no" turns the bill away (`addressMismatchMessage`).
- Lead capture and funnel (6 Oct 2026, after Meta brought visitors but almost no leads; ideas from Alex Hormozi's offer advice: speed to lead, lower the first ask, a niche): (1) "Prefer a hand with this?" (`EarlyContact`, top of "About your home", before the bill, optional, hidden once a bill is read): an email, a mobile or both (at least one) + `ConsentBoxes` and "Get in touch" (shown once they type) → `/api/follow-up` with `stage: "started"`: HubSpot note "Started their plan: contact now" (mobile-only contacts are named "New lead (mobile …)"), the lead is emailed straight away (`startedPlanEmail`: we'll be in touch, continue the plan, book a 15-minute call) and texted when Twilio is set up (`startedPlanSms`: a link to /book-a-call, Reply STOP; SMS isn't set up yet), staff are texted (`LEAD_ALERT_MOBILES` or `ALERT_MOBILES`, comma-separated, none by default; `newLeadStaffSms`, suburb only) and emailed (`newLeadEmail` kind "started"); Meta Contact + Google follow-up conversion; `earlyContact` in the flow state hides `BillLater`. (2) Landing pages (`domain/landing.ts`, tested: no amounts, figures or unprovable claims): `/battery` "Already have solar? Keep your sunshine for the evening." for homes with solar; `AddressEntry entry="battery"` starts the plan with "Would you like a battery?" yes (Recommended); `state.entry` puts "Landing page" in the HubSpot notes (reserve, follow-up, call). Its ad: `docs/marketing/meta-ads/battery-ad.md`. (3) Funnel steps for Meta (`domain/funnel.ts`, tested): each flow step sends one custom event the first time it's reached in a visit (`FUNNEL_EVENTS`: StepAddress, StepAboutHome, StepSystem, StepExtras, StepPartner, StepDate, StepReserve; `trackFunnelStep` from `AdTags`), no parameters, so Ads Manager shows where people stop.
- Ad landing page `/quote` (8 Oct 2026, after ads brought page views but no leads: the address + bill came before any contact details; `domain/quick-quote.ts` tested, `QuickQuote` in `components/consumer/quick-quote.tsx`, `/api/quote-lead` tested): wordmark-only header, then one tap a screen with a progress bar and Back: what they're looking for (solar + battery / a battery for my solar / solar / not sure), the power bill (Quarterly or Monthly, the four `SPEND_BANDS`), own the home (renters see a note and may continue; flagged "NO (renting)" for staff), the address (`AddressEntry onPick`, Victoria only), then first name + mobile (required) + optional email + `ConsentBoxes` → "Show my estimate". The server rebuilds everything from the answers (`cleanQuoteAnswers`, never figures from the browser): HubSpot contact (`followUpContactId`) + "Quick quote: call now" note with the answers and what was shown, staff email (`newLeadEmail` kind "quote", with the HubSpot line) and text (`ALERT_MOBILES`), and `quickQuoteEmail` when there's an email. Then "Thanks, <name>. Here's a first idea.": an indicative yearly saving (`quickEstimate`: `recommendSystem` on `billFromSpend` at average sunshine, rounded down to $50; none for a battery on existing solar, which needs the bill's exports), "See my full plan and price" (carries the address, spend estimate, battery choice and contact into the flow, so nothing is asked twice; `earlyContact` hides the other contact asks) and "Book a 15-minute call". Meta `Lead` + Google `follow-up` fire here once (`quoteTracked`); reserving later doesn't send a second Lead. `?for=battery` starts at the bill question with "A battery for my solar" chosen. Point ads at `/quote` (and `/quote?for=battery` for the battery ads).
- Guided brief (`domain/brief.ts` tested, `components/brief/`, `/brief/<key>`; ideas adapted from a separate project, nothing shared with it at run time, no other business named): a private link staff make on `/admin` → "Send a brief" (first name, mobile, optional email; `/api/admin/briefs`) and text from their own phone ("Open in Messages", `briefSms`: names RENUABL, Reply STOP). One thing a screen, progress "Step X of Y", Back, and "Don't have time to finish? Book a call" (jumps to the booking, noted "Brief not finished"): welcome → address (their roof from above) → timeframe → a held install date (same date rules and notes) → bill or spend range → what they're looking for → the house picture (`BriefHouse`, `houseShares` from the same `solarDay` rule as the savings: grid / solar used as it's made / battery after dark, average or winter day) → home on weekdays, ownership, the three Solar Victoria yes/no taps (VIC owner-occupiers only, criteria as on solar.vic.gov.au: income under $150,000, home under $3m, no solar in 10 years or rebate before; "looks eligible" only, Solar Victoria decides; switches the rebate on), roof, storeys, phase, shade, priority → products (leave it to us, or choose from `PANEL_RANGE`/`BATTERY_RANGE` with the same facts each, plus "Something else"; a preference only: prices use our usual products) → the price after rebates for solar only and solar + battery side by side (`useSystem().options`) → optional budget → questions or notes (+ Ask Revo) → book the 15-minute call (Mon–Sat times, best number, optional email, consent) → what happens next (`BRIEF_NEXT_STEPS`, times only where always true). Answers save as they go (`briefs` table, `/api/brief/[key]`); booking (`/api/brief/[key]/book`) checks the slot, puts every answer, what they saw and the staff-only Hot/Warm/Cold score with flags (`scoreBrief`: renting, heavy shade, no bill, products we don't list) in one HubSpot note, emails staff with the call as a calendar invite (`staffCallEvent`: who to ring and the number, staff only; `/api/consult` does the same) and sends the lead an invite when they gave an email; the "You're booked in" screen (and /book-a-call's) has Apple/Outlook and Google "Add to calendar" buttons (`call_slot` keeps the time for when the link is reopened). HubSpot from the start (`server/brief-hubspot.ts`): making the link adds the contact (first name, mobile, email; never overwrites) and a "RENUABL brief sent" note with the private link (`noteBriefSent`, contact id kept on the brief); a brief started and then left `BRIEF_UNFINISHED_HOURS` (3) without booking gets one "RENUABL brief not finished" note with the answers so far and the score, plus a staff email (`noteUnfinishedBriefs`, from the daily cron and when /admin opens, at most once a minute; `unfinished_noted_at`). The score is never shown to the customer. `/admin` lists briefs with status and score. Brief pages: noindex, no-referrer, no ad tags, `/brief` disallowed in robots.txt.
- Savings reveal (`domain/savings-preview.ts`, tested; `SavingsReveal` under the bill on "About your home"): once a bill is read, a "See how much I could save" switch shows a first yearly saving for solar only and solar with a battery (A smaller battery / The battery we'd suggest when expanding), from `savingsFor` (the same rule of thumb as `estimateOutcome`, no price needed), rounded down to $50, counting up with growing bars (no motion with reduced-motion). Always "a first estimate from your bill", never exact or guaranteed; the system step firms it up. Hidden when there's no saving to show.
- Sizing (`recommendSystem`), explained to customers under "How we worked this out" on the system step (`sizingExplanation`; keep it in step): Essential = the least solar that covers the home's yearly use at the year's average sunshine, never below the 5 kW minimum system (`ASSUMPTIONS.minSystemKw`); wanting a battery doesn't change it. Rule of thumb (`solarDay`): the home uses `ASSUMPTIONS.baseSelfConsumption` (40%, PLACEHOLDER; 30–40% is typical) of its solar as it's made, never more than its daily use; the rest of its use is after dark; the 60% surplus charges the battery, then exports. With a battery (Recommended, Maximum) it's sized for winter (`winterYield`: NASA's June–August sunshine against the year, else `ASSUMPTIONS.winterSunShare` 0.5): `panelsForBattery` fills the smallest hybrid inverter to `COSTING.maxArrayToHybridWithBattery` (150%, PLACEHOLDER until the CER/network wording is confirmed with Primero; 133% without a battery) whose winter surplus covers the after-dark use; the battery (`batteryForSolar`) is the smallest 8 kWh-module size whose 90% usable holds that winter after-dark use (one size up for backup). Savings (`estimateOutcome`) use the same `solarDay` at the year's average. Customers never choose panel counts or battery sizes. The options are Essential = solar only, Recommended = solar + battery, Maximum = the same solar and battery + a smart EV charger (the EV charger is only in Maximum; customers can still switch it on or off on the system step). Expanding existing solar: a smaller battery / the battery / the battery + EV charger (battery sized to the bill's after-dark purchases, `batteryNeeded`; top-up panels use `batteryReadySolar` headroom).
- The battery question is "Would you like a battery?" (a sale now, not "later"). Yes leads with Recommended, No with Essential.
- Existing solar (the bill shows exports): ask its size, or "I'm not sure", then "Replace or expand?". Expanding (or a known size) keeps the panels and sells a battery in every option, with extra panels only if exports can't fill it, and always shows `EXPAND_DISCLAIMER` (the inverter is unknown until the call), with an optional upload of the inverter's front and its model/serial sticker, which Claude reads (`/api/inverter`) for make, model and kW; photos aren't stored. Replacing sizes a new system to estimated real use (`realAnnualUse`, placeholder self-use share) and says it's an estimate. Logic: `src/lib/domain/existing-solar.ts`.
- One matched installer by default; alternatives only behind a secondary link. Customers never see the word "installer": they're **installation partners** (code, the installer portal and staff notes keep "installer"). Step 4 opens with "Finding your installation partner.", about 5 seconds of matching steps that are all true of the match, played once per address (`matchedPostcode`). Don't claim anything about timing the customer hasn't given yet.
- Install days (`scheduling.ts`, tested): weekdays only, never weekends or Victorian public holidays (`VIC_PUBLIC_HOLIDAYS`, listed to `HOLIDAYS_LISTED_UNTIL`: extend it every year, nothing later is offered; Grand Final Friday marked VERIFY). Where the Solar Victoria rebate may apply (new systems in Victoria, `installLeadDays`) the first day is `SOLAR_VIC_LEAD_DAYS` (14) days out, time for the approval, and every chosen date carries `REBATE_DATE_NOTE` ("subject to your rebate approval"); otherwise `LEAD_TIME_DAYS` (7), with `CAPACITY_DATE_NOTE` ("subject to installation calendar capacity"). `installDateNote` puts the right one on the date step, change-date sheet, reserve summary and confirmation page; emails, built on the server, carry `PROVISIONAL_DATE_NOTE` (both conditions). Days count from today's Melbourne date; every such day is offered (no partner calendars yet: `buildAvailability`). `/api/reserve` and `/api/reschedule` refuse weekends, holidays and anything under 7 days (`isBookableInstallDate`).
- Flow rewards (`domain/flow-moments.ts`, tested), one per step: the savings reveal after the bill; the system step is "Here's your system." with its cards fading in, estimated savings counting up (`CountUp`, `components/ui/count-up.tsx`) and "Good news: you're eligible for about $X in rebates" (`rebateReward`, sage card listing the rebates; the Solar Victoria "may also be eligible" line sits in it) — no price there; the date step leads with "Your system could be installed as soon as …" then the chosen day (`earliestInstallLine`, `pickedInstallLine`, sun card); the price comes last, on the reserve step's basket (`priceHeader`: "Your price, after $X in rebates"; extras no longer shows the system price); the WHOOP line there reads "Gift unlocked"; the confirmation page is "You're reserved!" with a one-off `Confetti` burst (CSS, hidden with reduced motion) and a black "Gift unlocked" card when a WHOOP was claimed. On phones the system step's savings and rebates card sits straight under the options.
- The customer picks the install day only; installers arrive 7am–9am (`INSTALL_ARRIVAL`), never a customer-chosen time. Lead times and the date notes: see Install days above (`useInstallLead`). The 15-minute confirmation call (confirmation, not sales) comes before the deposit.
- Intelligence is branded "Ask Revo", never labelled "AI". The chat is a conversation (`useRevoChat` in `services/revo-chat.ts`, kept in sessionStorage for the visit, shared by every Ask Revo button, "Start again" clears it): Revo opens with `REVO_GREETINGS` for the screen, remembers the last 10 exchanges, may ask one short question back, never asks for name, email, phone or address, and points pre-flow visitors to "start with your address". Revo also sits bottom right on the home page and guides (`page` companion, `REVO_PAGE_LINES`, bubble tucks away after 7 s). Revo (the mascot) is a persistent guide on every flow step (`RevoGuide` in `flow-shell.tsx`, the `revo` variant of `AskRenuabl`): a speech bubble with one line from `revoLine` (`src/lib/domain/revo.ts`, tested) that narrates the step and backs up each choice, plus the avatar; tapping opens Ask Revo for that screen (`revoContext`). Bottom right on desktop; above the sticky Continue on phones, where the bubble tucks away after 7 s (each new line brings it back). Lines stay short, never invent figures or claims. Answers come from Claude via `/api/ask` (`ASK_MODEL`, default `claude-sonnet-5`, using `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`), grounded only in `ASK_FACTS` and the customer's own answers (`src/lib/domain/ask-knowledge.ts`); keep those facts in step with these rules. Without a key, or if Claude fails, the reviewed canned answers in `src/lib/services/ask.ts` are used.
- No traditional website navigation (no Home / Energy / Savings / … link bar). Customer screens show only the wordmark and account; people move through guided steps, the My RENUABL home cards and the mobile bottom tabs.
- Reserving is free: the reserve step collects name, mobile and email (no payment) and sends the lead to HubSpot (`/api/reserve`, `HUBSPOT_PRIVATE_APP_TOKEN`). Every lead (reservation or "no bill yet") is also emailed to staff (`alertNewLead`, `newLeadEmail`; `LEAD_ALERT_EMAILS`, or the shorter `ALERT_EMAILS`, comma-separated, default martin@renuabl.com.au); it never blocks the lead. The customer is emailed the order breakdown and next steps (`src/lib/domain/emails.ts`, sent via Twilio SendGrid when `SENDGRID_API_KEY` is set, else Resend (`RESEND_API_KEY`), in `src/lib/server/email.ts`, with `EMAIL_FROM`) with an install-day invite; a call booked in the in-app calendar is noted in HubSpot and emailed with its own invite (`/api/call`). The confirmation page offers add-to-calendar (.ics + Google, `src/lib/domain/calendar.ts`) and only says an email was sent when it was. Emails never block a reservation. The $499 refundable deposit (`ASSUMPTIONS.deposit`) is taken after the confirmation call via Stripe's embedded checkout form on `/deposit` (`ui_mode: "form"`, API version `STRIPE_API_VERSION`, Stripe.js loaded from js.stripe.com/dahlia in `src/lib/services/stripe-form.ts`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`); setup steps in `STRIPE_INTEGRATION_TODO.md`. The partner calendar stays in-app (no ServiceM8).
