Nothing goes to the customer at publishing. **On the customer's page** the business's latest message is the greeting under the title (`ChatGreeting`, `greetingOf`), and the chat is a dock that stays at the foot of the screen as they scroll (`ChatDock`, a sticky strip of no height at the end of `ProposalPage`, so it also stays inside the business's preview), opened by its button, "Reply" or a link ending #chat; one `useCustomerChat` feeds both. **It looks and feels like iMessage** (Martin): own lines right in the main colour, the other side's left in grey, runs close together with a tail on the last of each (cut from `bg-canvas`, which the list must sit on), the time centred after a pause of over an hour (`timeBreaks`), "Sent" under the reader's latest line, a round box with the send arrow inside, the dock headed by an avatar of initials and the business's name.
- Roof designer deposits (11 Oct 2026; `domain/design/deposit.ts` tested, the deposit functions in `server/design-proposal-repo.ts`, `components/design/deposit-panel.tsx`; `design-deposit-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`): once a customer accepts a proposal that asks for a deposit, their page shows "Pay your deposit" (`PayDeposit`, under the acceptance, and whenever the link is reopened until the business marks it received). **RENUABL holds no money for another business, and never uses the homeowner `/deposit` flow for it.** Since 1 Nov 2026 a business may also take cards through its own Stripe account (see Card payments on proposals, below). The customer pays the business directly: by bank transfer (the account the proposal was sent with, the amount and a reference, each with a Copy button) or by card on the business's **own payment page** (`BrandKit.payLink`, "Card payment link" on Settings → Payment: https only, `cleanPayLink` refuses http, `javascript:`, bare words, local addresses; it opens in a new tab with a line saying who is paid and that no card is taken on this page). No link → bank transfer only; neither → "<business> will be in touch about how to pay the deposit". The amount is the one on the accepted copy (`terms.deposit`, from `depositOf`: to the cent, a percentage of or a fixed amount against `price.youPay`, the price after the federal rebate and **without** any Solar Victoria rebate, and the page says so); milestones are to the cent too. The reference (`newReference`, 8 letters and digits, the only one like it in the business, nothing to do with the link's token) is made at acceptance. "I've made the transfer" / "I've paid by card" (`POST /api/proposal/[token]` `action: "paid"`, rate limited, only a way that proposal offers, only once accepted) is kept as **what the customer said** (`deposit_said_way`, `deposit_said_at`), written on the record ("The customer says they paid … Not confirmed: check your account") and emailed to the business's `notify` addresses like an acceptance (`tellPaid`, `depositSaidEmail`: no bank details, no amount). **Said is never received**: only a member who may publish marks it (`PUT /api/design/jobs/[id]/deposit` `{received}`, `markDeposit`; "Not received" sets aside what the customer said so it is due again), each written on the record with who did it. The state lives in columns beside the fixed version (`deposit_ref`, `deposit_said_*`, `deposit_received_*` on `design_proposal_versions`); the accepted copy itself never changes. The deal and the proposal screen show it (`DepositLine`: due / the customer says paid, how and when / received). Bank details are shown only on the customer's own link and to the business's members, never in an email, a log or HubSpot. A new version published after acceptance has no deposit due until it is accepted in turn.
- **Charging businesses: Sol credits, users that follow the team, tax invoices (1 Nov 2026, Martin: everything needed to take payments and charge businesses).** **Sol credits** (`domain/design/sol-credits.ts` tested, `server/sol-credits.ts`, `design_sol_credits`): credits are spent by the answer (3 Nov 2026, Martin: Sol must make a profit, "heavily skewed to Sonnet", Opus "whenever it requires creativity or a better answer"; `domain/design/sol-routing.ts` tested): a **quick** answer (Sonnet 5.5, `SOL_QUICK_MODEL`) is 1 credit, a **deep** one (Opus 5.5, `SOL_MODEL`) 3 (`TIER_CREDITS`), and an answer researched online 2 more (`RESEARCH_CREDITS`). `routeQuestion` sends a question deep when the member presses **Think deeper** (under the latest quick answer, which asks it again), files are attached, it asks for writing someone will read (draft, reply, email…), judgement (why, should we, compare, objections…), care in so many words, or is long; else quick; a quick answer can hand itself up first (tool `think_deeper`, restarting on Opus) when the credits allow, so a question read as quick never gets a weaker answer than it needs; a deep one with too few credits left is answered quickly. Fixed tiers for the rest (`USE_TIER`): a customer's proposal chat, a call written up and a photo check deep; an inbox summary quick, a reply drafted deep (`inboxTier`); usage found, a service brief, team-chat work quick. Unasked channel joins, the drafted follow-ups (deep model) and the designer's command bar (quick) take no credit. Under each answer: "1 credit" or "Deep answer · 3 credits" (`costWords`). Uses: his panel and Today's box `chat`, @Sol in team chat `channel`, inbox `inbox`, a customer's proposal chat `customer`, a call written up `call-notes`, usage found in a file `usage`; each paid user brings `SOL_PER_USER` (150) a calendar month in the market's time (no roll-over), an old trial `SOL_TRIAL` (100) for the business, none without a plan; packs `SOL_PACKS` (250 $49, 1,000 $149, 3,000 $429, GST inclusive: about three times what credits cost, a credit costing about US$0.04 at list prices) never expire and are used once the month's are gone (`creditSource`). An answer is one row of `design_sol_credits` whose `change` is the credits it took (`spendSolCredit` with `credits`, `addToSolCredit` for a hand-up or research), with **what it really cost** kept on it (`tier`, `model`, `reason`, `cost_micros`, `usage`; `keepCost` from every call's usage, `domain/ai-costs.ts` tested: list prices a model, VERIFY when they change; `readWithClaude` takes `model` and `onUsage`); staff see it on `/admin` as **Sol's running cost this month** (`solCostsThisMonth`: answers, credits, US$ an answer and a credit, by tier). Check it against the packs before changing them. A credit is taken before Claude is asked (`takeSolCredit`/`withSolCredit`, under a lock) and given back when no answer comes; none left: Sol says `SOL_OUT`, the routes answer 402 `SOL_OUT_SHORT`, a customer's chat simply isn't answered by him. Like design tokens, **only stops anything with `BILLING_ENFORCED=true`**; until then an answer with none left is counted as `free`. Datasheet and invoice reading are not Sol answers and take no credit. **Users follow the team** (`syncSeats` in `server/design-seats.ts`): adding or removing a member sets the subscription's quantity with Stripe prorations (`setSeats`), and opening Billing catches up; Users and permissions says so (`SEAT_NOTE`). **Tax invoices**: every Stripe line carries an inclusive 10% GST rate (`gstTaxRate`, found by metadata `renuabl-gst`), packs make Stripe invoices (`invoice_creation`, `INVOICE_FOOTER` with RENUABL's ABN), and the Billing page lists the business's invoices from Stripe with **Tax invoice** (`/api/design/billing/invoices/[id]`: our own PDF, `taxInvoice` in `domain/design/billing-invoice.ts` tested, `server/tax-invoice-pdf.tsx`: "Tax invoice", Reburthed Pty Ltd trading as RENUABL and ABN, the buyer, GST as one eleventh of each line, only an invoice on the business's own Stripe customer) and Stripe's receipt or Pay link. **Billing details** (`design_billing_details`, `cleanBillingDetails`: name, ABN checked, email, address) are on every tax invoice and given to the Stripe customer (`syncBillingCustomer`). Settings → Billing (owner only): the plan, Sol credits with this month's use and packs, design jobs, invoices, billing details; the Stripe portal is for the card, billing period and cancelling. Stripe code for this is in `server/stripe-billing.ts`. Tested in `design-sol-credits-db.test.ts` (`npm run test:db`).
- **Card payments on proposals: the payment gateway for businesses (1 Nov 2026, Martin, reversing the earlier "no Stripe Connect" rule; RENUABL's fee 1%).** `domain/design/proposal-payments.ts` (tested), `server/design-connect-repo.ts` (`design_connect`, `design_proposal_payments`), `/api/design/connect`, `settings-card-payments.tsx` (Settings → Business → **Card payments from customers**, owner only), `pay-schedule.tsx`; `design-card-payments-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`. A business connects **a standard Stripe account of its own** (`createConnectAccount`, Stripe's onboarding pages, readiness followed by `account.updated` and re-read when Settings opens). The customer pays on Stripe's page as a **direct charge on the business's account**: the money is the business's, Stripe's processing fees and disputes are the business's, and RENUABL's `PLATFORM_FEE` (1%, GST inclusive) comes to RENUABL as an application fee. **What can be paid** is worked out on the server from the accepted copy (`paymentsDue`): the deposit first, then each milestone with the deposit taken off the first of them; **only the next amount due** (`cardPaymentFor`: refuses before acceptance, with cards off, or out of order). The customer's page shows **Payments** (`PaySchedule`, `id="pay"`): each amount, ticked when paid, and "Pay $X by card" for the next; bank transfer stays beside it (`PayDeposit` with `cardsHere`: bank only). The webhook (a second Stripe endpoint for **events on connected accounts**, `STRIPE_CONNECT_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, events `checkout.session.completed` and `account.updated`) keeps a payment once (`recordCardPayment`: only on that account's own business, only for the amount the version asks), marks a deposit received by `stripe` ("paid by card through Stripe" on `DepositLine`) and writes it on the customer's record. Settings lists RENUABL's fees by month (`feesByMonth`). Not built yet: an email to the business when a card payment comes in (Stripe's own emails cover it), a tax invoice from RENUABL for its fees, refunds from RENUABL (done in the business's Stripe Dashboard), surcharges. **VERIFY with the accountant**: GST on the 1% and its invoicing; with Stripe: the platform's Connect settings (standard accounts, AU).
- **RENUABL's plans for solar businesses, and the sign-up page (1 Nov 2026, Martin: "Pay less. Do more.")**. The address is the business's name made plain, a hyphen and a six-letter key from letters that can't be mixed up (`/primerosolar-k7m3qx`); the page answers only when both the key and the name part match, else a plain 404, so nobody finds a business's page by guessing its name. It shows the business's logo (the brand kit's, sent inline as a data address) over "Sign in to <its trading name>" and the usual sign-in (`SignInForm`: Google, password or a link), "Powered by RENUABL" under it; someone already signed in to a business goes straight to `/design`. Noindex, no referrer, no ad tags. It only brands the sign-in: anyone with an account signs into their own business from it. The address keeps the name it was made with until a new key is made. Seen in headless Chrome on the local example business (the card, the page with and without a logo, a wrong key 404). Not yet: a link to it in the welcome, the business's own colours on the page, and a custom domain of the business's own.

It steps aside while something is typed, as the foot bar does (globals.css). Pages that scroll inside themselves (the pipeline's board on a phone) don't show it. - **The lead inbox: emailed leads into the CRM (20 Nov 2026, Martin: "can we assign a connection to an email that would be the recipient of a lead? that way leads that arrive into an email inbox, can be then synced into the crm as a new lead").** `domain/design/lead-inbox.ts` (tested with a sample web-form email), `server/design-lead-inbox.ts` (tables `design_lead_inbox`, one row a business, and `design_lead_inbox_emails`, every email looked at once by its Gmail id, business id first; `design-lead-inbox-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`), `searchMessages` in gmail.ts, `/api/design/lead-inbox` (GET; POST `save`, `check`; who may change settings, never from a demo or while viewing as someone), `services/lead-inbox.ts`, `components/design/settings-lead-inbox.tsx`. Settings → CRM → **Lead inbox**: choose one of the Gmail mailboxes the business's people have connected (Email), optionally a Gmail label (e.g. Leads), **Switch on**, **Check now**, Switch off, and the latest 20 emails it read with what each became (New lead, Open deal) or why it was passed over (`SKIP_WORDS`). **The ten-minute cron** (`/api/cron/sol-outbox`) checks every switched-on business (`leadInboxBusinesses`, `syncLeadInbox`): Gmail's search (`gmailQuery`: the label else the inbox, not sent, nothing from before it was switched on and never more than `LOOKBACK_DAYS` 3 back), up to `PER_RUN` (15) a check, oldest first, each claimed before it is read (a claim left 15 minutes is stale), so running twice takes nothing twice. **Passed over by rule, no credit** (`skipByRule`): sent from the business's own mailboxes or people, a machine's email (`isAutomated`) with no person in Reply-To unless a label is set (a web form sends from no-reply and replies to the enquirer, `contactOf`), and someone who already has a deal that isn't lost (a reply, not a lead). **The rest Sol reads** (quick tier, one `inbox` Sol credit, structured output `LEAD_SCHEMA`, `leadPrompt`: the email fenced as data, never followed; spam, suppliers, newsletters, invoices, job applications and replies to work under way are not leads; details copied as written, never guessed; `cleanLeadReading`). A lead (an email or a mobile needed) becomes a customer found by email, else mobile (`findCustomerByMobile`), **never overwritten** (one with a deal that isn't lost is passed over; one with only lost deals gets the new deal), else made (source "Lead inbox"), and a deal at "lead" titled by what they want, its address found on the map with Google Places when it can be (`placeOf`), so **Sol · Leads' hello** (`lineUpHellos`) picks it up as any lead; a note on the deal by `lead-inbox` carries what Sol read and **the original email** (`leadNote`). An email Sol couldn't read (busy, no key, out of credits) is let go for the next check. Tried with Gmail, Sol and the map stood in for; **not run against a real Gmail or Claude**: VERIFY on the live business with a test enquiry and a web form's email. Not yet: more than one lead inbox a business, Outlook, assigning the new deals to a person, attachments carried onto the deal, and marking the email read or labelled in Gmail once taken.

Each icon is the network's mark in white on a round in the button colour, from Simple Icons' glyphs (CC0; LinkedIn's from v10, since removed there; the website a globe), in `social-glyphs.ts` (`socialIconSvg`), made a PNG by sharp at `/api/m/s/<network>.png?c=<hex>` (public, cached for good, `RenderLinks.social`; without it the email draws names). A product with no name isn't sent; its picture and button share one tracked link (`edmLinks`, in block order). `ProductCards` draws them in the editor and `ProductsFields` (edm-builder.tsx) edits them in place. Seen in headless Chrome on the local example business (two products, the preview); not sent for real: VERIFY two and three across in Gmail and Outlook on a phone. **A countdown block** (22 Nov 2026, Martin: "add a countdown timer block to the email builder"; `countdown` in `EDM_BLOCK_KINDS`, under Basics; `domain/marketing/countdown.ts`, tested in `countdown.test.ts`): a line over it (`COUNTDOWN_LINE`, "Offer ends in", changeable) and an end, a day and time in Melbourne's time (`endsAt` as "YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm", `cleanEndsAt`, `endsAtMs` with daylight saving). An email can't run anything, so **the timer is a picture drawn at each open**: `/api/m/t/<end in seconds>.gif?c=<hex>` (public, never cached, `RenderLinks.countdown`) draws the minute from that moment as an animated GIF, days, hours, minutes and seconds in tiles of the button colour (`countdownSvg`: the digits are strokes, so no font is needed on the server), played once and resting on its last frame; once ended, grey zeros. About 0.6 s of the server's time an open. Under it the units and the end in words (`endsWords`: "Ends Monday 30 November at 5:00 pm (Melbourne time)") are the email's own text, so an inbox blocking pictures still says when it ends; the picture's alt says it too. An email with a countdown and no end won't send (`contentProblem`). Seen in headless Chrome (the email at computer and phone width, the GIF served); **the block in the builder not seen**, and not sent for real: VERIFY in Gmail (its image proxy may keep a copy, so a second open can show the first open's time) and Outlook (Outlook for Windows shows a GIF's first frame only, which is still right at the open). **The business's logo at the top** (22 Nov 2026, Martin: "we need a clear upload business logo drop field"; `LogoField` in edm-builder.tsx, `EdmBrand.logoId`, `LOGO_HEIGHT` in edm.ts, tested in `edm-logo.test.ts`): where the email's head showed the business's name, the builder shows a dashed field, **Add your business logo**, to drop a picture on or press to choose one; with a logo, the logo with **Change logo** and **Remove** on hover. It is the business's marketing setting, not the email's (`saveMarketingLogo` → PUT `/api/design/marketing/settings` `{logoId}`, members who may publish; only a picture the business uploaded, `ownsImage`), so it heads every campaign and sequence email from then on; outside the builder's edit view the logo, or the name without one, shows. Uploaded as `kind` logo (`safeImage` keeps it PNG, so a see-through logo stays so). Sent at 44 px tall with the business's name as its alt text. The still is a picture the business uploads, else for a YouTube link the video's own thumbnail (`youTubeId`, `videoStillKey`: "yt-<id>"), drawn by `/api/m/v/<key>.jpg?c=<hex>` (public; cut to 16:9 and the button put on by sharp; an uploaded picture's cached for good, YouTube's a day; YouTube's fetched by the server through `safeFetch` from `i.ytimg.com`, `youTubeStill`). Without `RenderLinks.video` the plain picture, else the line alone. A video with no link, or with no picture and not on YouTube, won't send (`contentProblem`). The drawing seen on one of our photos; **YouTube's thumbnail not fetched here** (this container can't reach YouTube), and the block not seen in the builder: VERIFY a YouTube link and a Vimeo link with a picture on the live site, and in Gmail and Outlook. - **Dictation on a phone (21 Nov 2026, Martin: the microphone in Today's box did nothing on mobile).** `DictateButton` (dictate.tsx, used by every message box with a microphone): on a touch screen (`dictationOnPhone`) it listens once and stops by itself after a pause (`continuous` false; Android's Chrome repeats words in continuous mode and beeps on each restart, and iPhone's Safari won't restart without a tap), so a tap listens again; on a computer it carries on as before. Words still being said when it stops are kept (Safari often sends no final result). Every problem is said in a small bubble above the button for 7 s (`DICTATION_ERRORS`: microphone not allowed, Dictation off on the iPhone, no connection, nothing heard; a title never shows on a phone), and a browser with no speech recognition (Firefox, some iPhone browsers) says to use the keyboard's own microphone instead of a greyed button. If `en-AU` isn't offered it tries the device's own language. The message now says so (Siri switched on, Settings, Siri) and the next tap on the microphone opens the keyboard instead (`refused`, `openKeyboard`: the box the button sits in, focused from the tap, the cursor at the end), saying to use the keyboard's microphone. - **Improvements carried over from Primero (5–6 Nov 2026, Martin: Primero's session passed on its changes; each built in RENUABL's own code and look, nothing shared).**
  - **Sol never answers "Done."**: a turn where he used tools and said nothing is asked once more with no tools for words (`design-sol.ts`). **Several questions** in a customer's proposal chat get a paragraph each, naming the question (`MANY_QUESTIONS` in sol-customer.ts). **Follow-ups** (`followUpPrompt` in prepared.ts): their questions first, then what did they think, change anything, a call; about 60 words, one sentence a paragraph, no recap, never "no rush", never implying we didn't follow up or showing a mismatch in our records. **Any email from the deal or chat reply** counts as the follow-up due (`dueFollowUp` `lastTouch`), and **publishing isn't sending**: Sol drafts follow-ups only for a proposal that has reached the customer (`ProposalLine.sentOut`: opened, or written to since publishing), since the design tool publishes on every save. The **note-taker** keeps a neighbour's home, a past job or an example off the customer's system, listing anything unclear in toCheck. Publishing has `maxDuration` 60 and says when the server timed out.

## Brand system (from `docs/design/brand-and-desktop.webp`)

- Colours: Background `#FAF9F6`, Sand `#F0ECE7`, Sage `#D9E7DC`, Forest `#1E3A2E`, Charcoal `#1A1A1A`, White, plus a warm Sun accent (`bg-sun` `#F8E6C4` with `text-sun-ink` `#7A4F0E`; `text-sun-bright` on dark backgrounds) used only for small highlights: gift and "Free" lines, the odd sun icon, and on the home page the soft `.sun-glow` behind the hero, a thin gold underline under "sun" in the headline (`.sun-mark`, `--sun-line` #E9B949) and outlined gold step numbers on the home page (`border-sun-line text-sun-line-ink`; the pale butter fill there was replaced, it read as beige), and the "Learn with Revo" label. Never for buttons, prices or rebates (rebates stay `positive` green). WHOOP mentions (strip, badge, gift line, guide line) are black (`bg-ink text-canvas`) with an amber (`text-sun-bright`) gift icon. Forest is the Renewable/energy colour, Sage the Healthy home colour (package chips, the example plan, the Home Health teaser and results). They live as tokens in `src/app/globals.css`; use `bg-canvas`, `bg-surface`, `bg-sage`, `bg-sun`, `text-forest`, `bg-primary`, etc.

`PageSearch` in page-search.tsx, also Ctrl K or ⌘K anywhere: who's calling, matched as each digit goes in from the fourth, or by name, email or address, then their deals; `matches` in `domain/design/find.ts`, tested; anything typed that isn't a number also offers "Ask Sol", first when it reads as a question, `looksLikeQuestion`, tested, and choosing it opens his panel with it asked, `SOL_ASK`); the sections in one pill in the middle (**folded since 8 Nov 2026, Martin**: the sections are the engine Sol works on, not the surface, so a retailer's CRM, Procurement, Installation and Service sit behind a layers icon between the designer's round and Sol's, `SectionFold` in section-fold.tsx; it opens sideways in place after the pointer rests on it for 0.15 s (or on a tap, or on keyboard focus), folds again 0.25 s after the pointer leaves, a tap elsewhere or Escape, stays open while a page inside one of them is on show, and can be pinned open (`renuabl.design.sections-pinned` on the device); - **Sol, the business's assistant (1 Nov 2026, Martin: Primero's Lance copied across and named Sol; wave 2 of the copy).** `domain/design/sol.ts` (tested: turns, preferences, reminders in the market's time, what a member sends, "#" deal lookup, the tools' answers, `solSystem`), `sol-charts.ts` and `sol-diagrams.ts` (as Primero's: one-series charts; diagrams written as SVG and rebuilt from an allowlist by `cleanSvg`, shown as a picture), `server/design-sol.ts` (Claude with tools, `SOL_MODEL`, default claude-opus-5-5, needs `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`; without it he says so), `server/design-sol-repo.ts` (table `design_sol`: one row a member, business id first, their turns (latest 80), preferences and reminders; a reminder that has fallen due is said in their chat the next time the row is read, which the rail does every minute), `/api/design/sol` (GET the state; POST chat, seen, clear, remind, forget-reminder, forget-pref; chat rate limited), `services/sol.ts`. **Each member's conversation is their own.** He answers from the business's deals **as that member may see them** (`viewerOf`, the same facts as the canvas, `canvasFacts`) and acts only through tools: find_deal, deal_details (customer, deal, details typed in, latest activity), deals_at_stage, price_lookup (**only for a member who sees costs**), draft_email, remember/forget a preference (sign-off, name, tone), add/set/list/remove a reminder, show_chart, show_diagram. **He never sends anything**: an email is a draft card ("Draft email · not sent") whose "Open in the deal's email" hands it to that deal's Email tab (`DRAFT_KEY` in sessionStorage, `?tab=email`) for the member to check and send, or Copy. Answers are short first, "Want more detail?" offered as a button. **Copy and Pin on every message** (1 Nov 2026, Martin, as Primero's): Copy puts the message's words on the clipboard; Pin marks the turn (`pinned`, `withPin`, POST `do: "pin"` with `at` and `role`): pinned turns show in a **Pinned** strip at the top of the chat (a press scrolls to the message, the cross unpins), are kept past the history trim (`keptTurns`) and survive Clear. The system prompt is built once before the tool loop and reused for every step (as Primero's fix: rebuilding it mid-turn makes the API reject the turn's thinking). Screens (`sol-chat.tsx`, `sol-dock.tsx`, `sol-icon.tsx` the rayed sun with a spark, `sol-thinking.tsx`, `sol-visuals.tsx`, `dictate.tsx` + `speech.ts`): the box takes words, the microphone (the browser's own recognition), up to three PDFs or pictures, and "#" to pick a deal; his page `/design/sol` (reminders and preferences beside the chat; under More on a phone); **Sol's mark** (`SolIcon` for small places, `SolMark` the lit one on graphite, sol-icon.tsx; 3 Nov 2026, Martin: the spark in a ring of rays read as any AI's, cookie cutter, not tied to RENUABL; a speech bubble was tried the same day and didn't read as AI): just a sun (a large gold-to-orange disc, no rays, in his round; small places use the same shape, the disc low left with the two stars above it right: `SolIcon` in the text colour, `SolBadge` in his own colours), and the AI's stars (`SolStars`, two sparkles) floating diagonally above his round and outside it; his unread count sits at the round's lower right; no rays anywhere round him: the sun itself (10% larger) beats like a heart, two quick beats and a rest with its glow brightening (`sol-heartbeat`, still with reduced motion), a subtle signal to engage. **His round in the nav** (see the frame) opens **his panel**; the rail down the right (`SolRail`) is now team chat, Sales this month and Proposals signed (`SolDock`, 26rem, `--sol-w`: from 1440px the page narrows to make room, `html.sol-docked`; below that, and on a phone, it lies over the page; Escape closes; kept open from page to page for the tab). The day and weather pill goes away when the top bar is short of room (under 1536px, or under 1920px with the panel open). Left out from Primero's Lance: emailing the team, feature requests, the customer inbox and routines (Gmail comes in wave 7), sending on his own. **Past conversations** (1 Nov 2026, Martin: a clock wound back in the chat's corner; `domain/design/sol-history.ts` tested, `components/design/sol-history.tsx`, `design_sol.past`, POST `past` / `past-open` / `new` / `resume` / `forget-past`): the History button on the right of the chat's header shows **Start a new chat** and the past conversations by day, each by the member's first question (`titleOf`); one opens as it was with **Continue this conversation**, which files the current one away in its place; the bin forgets one. A new chat files the current one away (only once the member has said something, `worthKeeping`) and carries its pinned messages on (`freshTurns`); up to `MAX_PAST` (40) kept. **After a while away the chat opens clean** (18 Nov 2026, Martin: "the sol chat should start as a new chat after say 15 minutes. i hate the busy ness of the previous chat"; it was 4 hours with a question over the old chat): 15 minutes after the last message (`RETURN_GAP_MINUTES`, `askToStartAfresh`, looked at again when the tab comes back into view) his panel and page show the empty chat with its suggestions and one quiet line, **Continue your last chat: <its first question>** (kept for the tab for that last message, `renuabl.design.sol-carried`); the old conversation and its pinned strip are hidden, and the first thing asked files it away (`newSolChat`, pinned messages carried on) and starts a new chat.

it was makers' and official sites only, `MAKER_SITES` is now named to him as the makers to try first), searched from Melbourne (`SEARCH_FROM`), `SOL_WEB_SEARCHES` 20 and `SOL_WEB_FETCHES` 10 an answer (19 Nov 2026, Martin, after Sol said his "web search limit ran out after one search": "he shouldnt"; it was 5 and 4, now only a guard against a search that never ends, and his instructions say never to mention a search or tool limit, to search again with other words, and to say what he confirmed and what he couldn't); he prefers official bodies and networks, then makers, wholesalers and the CEC, treats a forum as experience only, gives figures and contact details exactly as the page shows, and never fills a gap. **Sources**: the newer search tool filters results in code and often leaves no citations, so he ends an answer with "Sources:" and the addresses he relied on; the server takes the list off his words (`takeSourcesList`), keeps only pages he really saw in that answer (`reliedOn`, matched by `urlKey`: a made-up address is dropped), adds pages cited or read whole and saved answers recalled (`memoryUsed`), and puts them on the turn (`SolTurn.sources`), shown as **the sources icon** under the answer (`SolSources`: globe or bookmark, the count, opening each page's title and site, from memory with the day it was found, links open in a new tab with no referrer); a team chat line, being words only, keeps the plain list (`sourcesLine`). **Memory, for the whole business**: `recall_knowledge` first for any such question (`recallKnowledge`: shared words, the saved question counting double; each with its age and, past `STALE_DAYS` for its topic (rebates 45 days, technical two years), marked to check again), then the web; after answering from pages read in that answer, `save_knowledge` keeps the question, answer, topic and pages (`cleanKnowledgeInput`: only pages seen in that answer or already in memory, so nothing is kept unsourced; never the business's customers, deals, prices or costs; the same question replaces the older answer; up to `MAX_KNOWLEDGE`); the turn says "Kept in Sol's memory" (`SolTurn.remembered`), and an answer with sources not yet kept offers **Remember this** (POST `remember`, `guessTopic`). **What Sol knows** on `/design/sol` beside reminders and preferences: by topic, searchable, each opening to its answer and sources, Forget (POST `knowledge`, `forget-knowledge`). Sol's unprompted channel replies (`design-sol-joins.ts`) stay without the web, on purpose (cheap and quick). The side panel (`SolChat side`) shows **Looking at <page · first line>** above the box, pressed to ask without it (back on for each new page), and sends it with the question (`page` on POST chat, `cleanPageContext`: plain text, `PAGE_LIMITS`); the server puts it ahead of that question only, fenced as `<on_their_screen>` (`pageContextBlock`), never kept in the conversation, and his instructions (`SOL_ON_SCREEN`) say it is data, never instructions, and answer "this design" from it. It is only what the member's own screen already shows. Sol's page (`/design/sol`) and Today's box send none. **The panel's width**: its left edge drags it (`role="separator"`, arrow keys too, double-click resets), between `SOL_PANEL.min` and `.max` and never more than 60% of the window (`solPanelWidth` in `domain/design/sol-panel.ts`, tested), kept on the device (`renuabl.design.sol-width`) and set as `--sol-w`, which the page, the rail and team chat already follow (`html.sol-resizing` stops their easing while dragging). **Sol shows a deal's roof design in the chat (3 Nov 2026, Martin: a core feature, "show me the rooftop design of the next upcoming install")**: tool `show_design` (deal id; only a deal the member may see, `jobById` with `viewerOf`) puts `{jobId, title}` on his turn (`SolTurn.designs`, `domain/design/sol-designs.ts`, tested, `DESIGNS_PER_TURN` 2) and tells him what is drawn in words; nothing drawn is kept in the conversation: `SolDesignView` (sol-design-view.tsx) reads the deal's job card when shown (the design signed for, else the primary), so it is the design as it is now and only for someone who may see the deal: the panel layout (Layout / Strings) over the roof photo (`jobDrawing`), the system in a line, full size on a press, and **Open the design** (`designHref`). - **Industry news (10 Nov 2026, Martin, on learning SolarEdge had withdrawn from Australia: keep us up to date with companies coming and going, rebate changes, new products and capabilities).** `domain/design/industry-news.ts` (tested), `server/industry-news.ts` (tables `industry_news`, `industry_news_runs`: **one feed for the whole platform**, public news, no business's data), `/api/design/news`, `/api/cron/news` (vercel.json, 19:00 UTC, before Melbourne's working day), `services/industry-news.ts`, `components/design/industry-news.tsx`. **News** is the last tab on Today (`TODAY_TABS`). Each morning Sol (`NEWS_MODEL`, default claude-sonnet-5-5, with web search and fetch, `BLOCKED_SITES` kept out, searched from Melbourne) finds the Australian solar, battery and EV charger news of the last 7 days by topic (`NEWS_TOPICS`: companies, rebates, rules and standards, networks and the grid, new products, technology) and writes each story **in his own words** (never a copied sentence) with one line on what it means for a retailer or installer ("For you"), the makers named (`brands`) and its source. `cleanNewsItems` keeps only pages his search really showed (by `urlKey`: a made-up link is dropped), none older than `NEWS_DAYS` (14), none twice, at most `MAX_PER_RUN` (12). One reading at a time, claimed under an advisory lock (`newsDue`: every `NEWS_REFRESH_HOURS` (20), a failed one retried after an hour); opening the tab starts one when the cron hasn't (after the answer). **RENUABL pays, no Sol credit**; each reading's cost is kept on `industry_news_runs.cost_micros`. The tab says when a reading failed and that every story is to be checked at its source. **A story's makers against the business's own price lists** (10 Nov 2026, Martin): the route sets each story's `brands` against the products on the reader's lists (`onYourLists`, `namesMaker`: whole words, "SolarEdge" or "Solar Edge", never a general word like "solar" or a maker under three letters alone) and adds `mine` (how many products, up to three names; never a cost, never stored); the story then carries "2 SolarEdge products on your price lists" and the tab says how many stories touch the lists. **Posts from businesses** (10 Nov 2026, Martin: "allow users to post, making it somewhat like a social feed"; every business sees them, with comments, likes, a photo or a link, and Report): `domain/design/feed-posts.ts` (tested), `server/design-feed-posts-repo.ts` (tables `design_feed_posts`, `design_feed_comments`, `design_feed_likes`, `design_feed_reports`; `design-feed-posts-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`), `/api/design/posts` (GET, `?reported=1` for staff; POST a form of text, link, photo), `/api/design/posts/[id]` (GET comments; POST like, unlike, comment, uncomment, report, remove, keep) and `/picture?which=photo|link`, `services/feed-posts.ts`, `components/design/feed-posts.tsx` (`PostComposer`, `PostCard`). The box sits at the top of the News tab and posts are merged with Sol's stories by day (`mergeFeed`), with a **Posts** chip. **Every business reads every post** (the one place businesses talk to each other in words of their own): words go through `publicWords` (emails, phones, links and street addresses taken out, `POST_RULES` says so), a link goes in its own field (`cleanPostLink`: public https only; its title and preview picture read after posting with `linkPreview`), a photo is redrawn (`safeImage`); a reader sees "Sam · Sunrise Solar", the business's kind and when, never an email or a business's id. A post is removed by its writer, their business's owner or staff (`mayRemove`); a business reports a post once, never its own, staff are emailed on the first report (`tellStaff`), and reports from `REPORT_HIDE` (3) businesses hide it from all but its writer and staff, who see **Reported** on the tab with Keep it / Remove it. Installers and inspectors reach the feed at `/design/news` (Industry news in the profile menu; `news` and `posts` in `COMMON_API`). Rate limited: 20 posts, 120 comments and 30 reports an hour. Not yet: notifications of comments on your post, editing a post, more than one photo. Not yet (stories): the makers on designs and open deals, Sol answering from the news, staff seeing reading costs on /admin. **How sure each story is** (10 Nov 2026, Martin: say whether a claim is verified or speculative, and cite the government site, not Finder): each carries `standing` (`NEWS_STANDINGS`: **Confirmed** by the body responsible, **Reported** by credible news, **Unconfirmed** speculation, leaks or proposals) and Sol's one line on why (`standingNote`), shown as a pill and a line under the summary. A rebate, rules or network story (`OFFICIAL_TOPICS`) is confirmed only from an official page (`isOfficialSite`: .gov.au, AEMO, Standards Australia, Solar Accreditation Australia, Victoria's networks), else it reads Reported with `NOT_OFFICIAL_NOTE` (`cleanStanding`), and is never taken from a comparison site (`NOT_FOR_OFFICIAL_TOPICS`: Finder, Canstar, SolarQuotes…); the prompt sends him to the official page first and never to a seller's pricing page. In tries Sol often couldn't open cer.gov.au, so rebate stories mostly come as Reported from RenewEconomy or the ABC. **More stories, products every day, and a picture on each** (10 Nov 2026, Martin): Sol is asked for `AIM_STORIES` (10) or more across the topics with at least two product stories when there are any (`MAX_PER_RUN` 15; 14 searches and 5 page reads a reading, about US$1.60 a reading); every Australian network is an official site (`OFFICIAL_SITES`). Each story's picture is its page's own preview picture (`pageImageUrl`: og:image, then twitter:image, https only), fetched with every hop checked (`server/safe-fetch.ts`: public addresses only, looked up as the connection is made), taken only when its own bytes say JPEG, PNG or WebP (`sniffImage`), redrawn as a 720 × 405 JPEG with `sharp` and kept on the story (`industry_news.image`, `image_tried`), so it loads from our database and no reader's browser calls the news site; `fillPictures` runs after each reading and, for stories not yet looked at, after the tab is read; served at `/api/design/news/<id>/image` to signed-in members (`newsPicture`). Every story carries a picture across its top (10 Nov 2026, Martin: the feed blended into itself): its page's own, else a drawn band in its topic's colour with its mark (`StoryPicture`, `TOPIC_ART`), never a picture taken from elsewhere; summaries past 240 characters fold to three lines with More, and stories and posts sit apart with room and a soft shadow. The feed sits in a panel outlined in the Marketplace's blue (`ring-market`) with an "Across RENUABL" pill, and the News tab is blue on Today (10 Nov 2026, Martin: it is the industry's feed, not the business's own work). **The news at the product level** (10 Nov 2026, Martin: recalls, approved-list changes, makers leaving, new models, maker notices and supplier price moves, "all of the above is useful"; `domain/design/product-watch.ts` tested, `server/design-product-watch.ts`): three more kinds of story, **Recalls and safety** (productsafety.gov.au first, the electrical safety regulators, the maker's own notice), **Approved products** (models added to or taken off the approved lists rebates depend on; in `OFFICIAL_TOPICS`, `cleanenergycouncil.org.au` counted official for it, VERIFY who keeps each list), and **Maker notices** (firmware, installation and safety bulletins, warranty and support changes); every story now names the exact models it is about (`models`) and, for a new model, those it replaces (`replaces`), never guessed. Each business's feed sets every story against its price lists (`onTheLists`) and the systems on its sold and installed deals this member may see (`inTheirSystems` over `dealKits`: the as-built's models when recorded, else the design signed for or the primary, and the product lines typed on the deal); a story naming models matches **by model only** (`namesModel`: five or more letters and digits with a digit, or eight without), one naming only makers by maker. The card says "This model is in 2 installed systems of yours" with each customer linking to their deal, and what is on the price lists (or which product a new model replaces); a recall, delisting, maker notice or maker leaving that touches the business is **Needs a look** (`needsALook`: a warning band on the card and a banner at the top opening **Your products**, the stories and price moves that touch the business). **Supplier price moves**: saving a price list keeps what moved against the business's prices before (`priceMoves`: 3% and $1 or more, the biggest six, how many are new; `design_price_moves`), shown on its News tab only to members who see costs ("Prices moved on AWM November.xlsx: 9 up, 2 down."). Only a retailer's deals are read (an installer or inspector has none). It uses the plain search tool (`web_search_20250305`, 8 searches): the newer one ran its searches in code, took past the time limit and hid the pages it saw. **Both readings**: the pages Sol's code printed now count as seen (`pagesIn` reads addresses in the tool results' text), which had been dropping most of the solar reading's stories too; a reading stops starting new turns near the 300-second limit (`READING_BUDGET_MS`, `LAST_TURN_MS`), each call given what is left. - **Team chat (1 Nov 2026, Martin: Primero's team chat copied across; wave 3).** `domain/design/team-chat.ts` (tested), `server/design-team-repo.ts` (tables `design_team_lines`, `design_team_files`, `design_team_seen`, business id first), `/api/design/team` (GET a room or `?count=1`; POST JSON say / seen / edit / delete / react, or a form with files) and `/api/design/team/files/[id]`, `services/team-chat.ts`, `components/design/team-chat.tsx`; `design-team-chat-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`. A business's own members talking: three channels the whole business shares (Deals, Installations, Service) and a direct conversation between any two members (`dmRoom`; only ever read by its two people, and only with another member of the same business, `mayUseRoom`). **Never seen by customers.** Who wrote a line is the signed-in member, set on the server. In a channel, **@someone** tags a member (the box offers names after @; each tagged member is emailed from RENUABL with the words and a link that opens the chat on that channel, `teamTagEmail`, `?team=<room>`, only when email and `SITE_URL` are set up) and **@Sol** asks Sol, who answers in the channel as the member who tagged him (`solAnswersInChannel` in design-sol.ts, after the response: he sees only what they may see, with the channel's last 12 lines; the question and answer also go into that member's own conversation with him, and an email he writes waits there as a card; he never puts a customer's phone or email in a channel). **#** drops a deal in as a card (read from the business's deals on the server, only deals the writer may see; **hidden from a member who can't see that deal**). Files and pictures go with a line (paperclip, paste a screenshot, or drop on the room; pictures, PDFs, Word, Excel, CSV, text; up to six, 4 MB together, `teamFilesProblem`; kept in Postgres, served only to someone who may read the room they were sent in, pictures shown inline, the rest downloaded, sandboxed). Hover a line to react (six quick, 32 in all, `TEAM_EMOJIS`) or, for your own, to change it ("edited") or delete it with its files. Unread counts others' lines since the room was last open (a room never opened counts the last day's, `unreadByRoom`); what is on screen is read. It asks every 10 seconds while open (`TEAM_POLL_MS`), more often while Sol is answering ("Sol is answering…"). **Where**: a button on the rail under Sol, with the unread count, opens it in a window beside the rail over the page (drawn on the page body, since the rail is moved by a transform); with Sol's panel open the rail and the window move left of it (`.sol-rail`, `.team-pop`); `/design/team` is its own page (under More on a phone). Own lines in graphite on the right, others' grey, Sol's in the pale sun colour with his mark.
- **Today (1 Nov 2026, Martin: Primero's Today copied across; wave 4; it replaces the canvas overview).** `/design` signed in is `TodayPad` (`today-pad.tsx`, rules in `domain/design/today-pad.ts`, tested): one pad with **Today**, **Calendar** and **Pipeline** across its top (Today carries the count of things that need the business), and **Your day** in the corner (the sun keeping time on the day's list, `YourDay`, with the journey under it). **Today is one chat with Sol** (3 Nov 2026, Martin: the pad and Sol's box felt like two things; one large chat screen, dynamic and real time, not a software landing pad): it opens on Sol's round, the business's name, the greeting and the headline (`nextHeadline`) in the middle of the screen, with **Show me my day** and Sol's box under them. Pressing it, or asking anything, folds the greeting away; Sol thinks (`SolThinking`), then writes the day up as you watch (`SolDay`, his words from `solDayScript`, tested): each line typed out with his caret, and between them each part put in place as he gets to it, coming in out of a soft blur with a warm light passing across once (`Made`): **Up first** (the most pressing thing from `nextUp`, large, with its one button), **Before you call**, the rest **to action** (five, appearing one by one, then "N more"), what he drafted (`ReadyForYou`), and the day so far in a sentence; "Show it all" finishes at once, reduced motion shows it still. Questions go in the same column (their words right, his answer typed out left), the box docks at the foot, and asking from Calendar or Pipeline comes back to it. The parts: **Before you call** (`beforeYouCall`: the first item's customer, the deal in a line, the last thing that happened with them and what is still open: no phone, no email, no owner, roof not designed, no install date, nothing logged; with Call as a tel: link, Email (the deal's Email tab) and Open deal) and **Your day so far** (`daySoFar`: the calls, emails, notes, proposal updates and deal moves this member logged today, from `Overview.mine`, the market's day). **Calendar**: a day's installs (from the crew's start, `INSTALL_DAY`) and this member's own reminders from Sol (`agendaFor`), stepping through the working days (`nextWorkingDay`: weekends and Victorian public holidays passed over), with a link to the install calendar. **Pipeline**: **Board** (`pipelineBoard`: lead, designed, proposal sent and won, each with its count, value and six latest deals, "N more" to Deals; 5 Nov 2026, Martin: Sol and his helpers do the reaching out, so the board stays macro and shows it at a glance: a card carries an envelope once an email has gone from the deal and an orange eye on its corner once the latest has been opened, `MailBadge`, the words in its title, from `mailMark`/`mailMarkWords` in `domain/design/email-opens.ts`, tested, via `mailMarks` in design-pulse-repo.ts and `/api/design/mail-marks`; every email sent from a deal counts, Sol's follow-ups included; an open is a guide, not proof) or **Numbers** (`Numbers` in canvas-pad.tsx: new leads today, sales today and this month, installations today, sales closed this month). What is asked in Sol's box is the same conversation as his panel. `?view=` still opens Sales, Upcoming or Completed on its own.

- **Sol's sales agents on the pipeline (5 Nov 2026, Martin: phase two, Sol reaching out to customers to close them, with the team at ease that he is at work and doing the right work).** `domain/design/sol-agents.ts` (tested), `server/design-sol-agents.ts` (table `design_sol_agents`, one row a business: its settings), `/api/design/sol-agents`, `services/sol-agents.ts`, `components/design/sol-agents.tsx`. **One Sol wearing a hat for each stage, never four characters**: on Today's Pipeline board each column's header has a Sol round (`AgentRound`: Sol · Leads, Designs, Proposals, Won, `AGENTS`; an orange count of emails lined up, red for conversations that need a person, a green dot while it is on; dimmed while that agent isn't working yet). Only **Sol · Proposals** works today (his drafted follow-ups, Ready for you's items); the others say what is coming (`soon`). **One outbox behind them**: Ready for you's items, each with its stage and when it goes (`sendAt`: `MIN_REVIEW_MINUTES` (30) after it was ready, inside the business's hours (5 Nov 2026, Martin: set send windows meant nothing to anyone; `SEND_HOURS`: **Business hours**, weekdays 9 am to 5 pm, the default, or **Longer hours**, weekdays 8 am to 7 pm; never weekends or Victorian public holidays, `withinHours`, `nextOpen`); one whose time passed out of hours waits for the hours to open, so nothing reaches a customer at night). **Each deal card carries a chip** (`OutboxChip`, `chipWords`): "Sol emails in 1h 52m" (orange in the last half hour), "Sol drafted · review" while its agent is off, held, or **Needs you · they wrote** (the customer's line is the conversation's latest: Sol steps aside). **Pressing a round opens the stage's card** (`AgentCard`, over the page; 5 Nov 2026, Martin: it was hard to understand, so just what this is, what it does with an on and off, and when): what this is (`Agent.what`), **What it does** (`Agent.job`) with its **on/off switch** and one line on what on and off mean, **When he sends** (the hours), then Next up (each email opens to its words and why, with **Send now**, Edit, Hold / Let Sol send it, Skip, Open deal), Needs you and Sent by Sol this week. **An agent sends on its own only once the owner switches it on** (`AgentSettings.on`, the stages switched on, none to start: the drafts wait for a press; older settings with one `sending` switch and pauses are read as the same agents on, `cleanAgentSettings`), never while held (`outboxState`, `agentOn`), only while the deal is still at the agent's stage and the follow-up still due, and only with email set up. Sending happens two ways: reading the outbox (the board asks each minute) sends what is due after the answer (`fireDue`, only inside the hours), and `/api/cron/sol-outbox` runs every hour (vercel.json) for every business with an agent on (`sendingBusinesses`), so emails go in business hours with nobody logged in. An email goes as the deal's own does (`sendOutboxItem`: RENUABL's sender in the business's name, replies to the brand email or the owner, written on the record by `sol`, opens tracked, the follow-up marked done), claimed before sending so it goes once; a failure puts it back held. The hours need `publish`; switching an agent on or off, the owner. Not tried against a real email service from this machine: VERIFY one send before switching an agent on for real customers.
- **The team chat on every deal (8 Nov 2026, Martin: "the request pad next to a deal"; in place of internal notes and emails left for teammates on a deal page)**: `domain/design/deal-team.ts` (tested), `server/design-deal-team-repo.ts` (table `design_deal_lines`, business id first; `design-deal-team-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`), `/api/design/jobs/[id]/team` (GET the lines and the people to @mention, `?ask=<line>` one request; POST `{text}`, `{done, undo?}`), `services/deal-team.ts`, `components/design/deal-team-chat.tsx` (`DealTeamChat`, full or `pad`), `design-brief.tsx`. **For the business's own people and Sol, never the customer**, and a different thing from the customer's proposal chat (`design_messages`); the one global team chat (channels, direct messages) stays. **Who**: everyone who may see the deal (every repo function a member reaches reads the deal through `jobById` with their viewer, so `allDeals` and owners hold; a deal they can't see answers 404); an @mention notifies the member (`notifyMentions` with `tab: "team"`, which as on notes also lets them see the deal) and emails them a link to `?tab=team`. **Where**: the deal's **Team** tab (after Activities, `?tab=team`), and **the pad** beside each card on Today's deal feed (wide screens a column on the right, below on narrow; the latest lines with an open request kept in view, `padOf`, and a box; the feed carries the pad's lines and open requests, `padsFor`). **Sol writes two kinds of line** through `solPostsOnDeal` (never a member's functions): a **note** (first person, only when something changed or he learned something, never a daily status) and a **request** (`DealAsk`: what he wants, why as the customer's own words with where and when (`AskQuote`), one action button, Mark done / Open again by anyone who sees the deal; `ASK_TYPES` say whose job it is: design for a designer, price for whoever sets prices, shown as a label only for now). One open request of a kind a deal (checked under the deal's lock). Open requests count on the feed's **Needs you** (`needsYou` → "Sol needs a design") and its caption. Sol's notes and requests, and a request marked done, are also written on the deal's record as notes (by `sol`); the team's own lines aren't (they would crowd it). **The first trigger** (`solAsksForDesigns`, run as the feed is read, at most every five minutes a business; by rule, **no Sol credit**): a lead with no roof design and no kW (a deal moved to Designed has one somewhere) whose customer has said what they want (their own lines in the proposal chat first, then the deal's notes and call notes, by `wantsFrom`'s words: battery, panels, bill, EV, roof…; voicemails left out) gets one design request quoting them (`designAskFor`), never a second one for that deal even once done. Its button, **Open the designer with this brief**, opens the designer drawer on the deal (`briefHref`: `/design/roof?job=<id>&ask=<line>`) with `DesignBrief` over it (what he wants, the quotes, Mark done). **@Sol on a deal** (`solAnswersOnDeal` in design-sol.ts, after the response): he answers in the deal's chat in the shared view (no costs, nobody's private history), given the deal's facts, its latest record and customer chat lines and the chat before it (all things every reader of the chat already sees on the deal), and the question also goes into the asker's own conversation with him; one `channel` credit, as @Sol in a team channel. **One conversation shown in three places (8 Nov 2026, Martin: a message on a deal on Today reaches the person in the side panel's team chat, their reply shows in both, and # and a deal's name opens its chat where it left off)**: stored once, shown on the feed card's pad, the deal's Team tab and the team chat window (`team-chat.tsx`); the window has a **Deals** section above the channels (`DealChatsList`: the deal chats this person follows, unread first then the latest line, `dealChatOrder`, each with the customer, the latest line and its count; `/api/design/team?deals=1`, `dealChats`) and its room for one is `deal:<job>` (`dealRoom`/`jobOfRoom`), drawn by `DealTeamChat fill` with Open deal. **Following** (`design_deal_follows`; `isFollowing`: a choice wins, else the deal's owner, who follows once the chat has something in it): writing in it follows it, being @mentioned or being the one a request of Sol's is for (`askFollowers`: design to the designers, else the owners; a price to whoever may change prices) follows it unless they chose not to, and a **Following** toggle on the full chat follows or stops (`followDeal`); only deals the member may see are listed (`listJobs` with their viewer). **Read once, read everywhere** (`design_deal_seen`, `dealUnread`: others' lines since last open, never opened the last day's): the full chat (Team tab or window) reads it on each look (`?seen=1`), writing reads it, the pad doesn't; the rail's team chat count adds the deal chats followed (`/api/design/team?count=1`), and a read anywhere refreshes the count, the window's list and the feed (`DEAL_TEAM_CHANGED`). **# in the window**: the # by Deals takes a customer or deal name (`matches` on `listJobs`) and opens that deal's chat, following it (`openDealChat`, POST `{open: true}`); a deal card dropped into a channel line has **Open its chat**. **Resizing and short asks (12 Nov 2026, Martin: the deals' names were cut off; "its too much. just ask for whats needed")**: team chat's window is dragged from its left edge, foot or the corner between them, and its list of conversations from its right edge (`Grip` in team-chat.tsx; `teamWindowSize`, `teamListWidth` in `domain/design/team-window.ts`, tested; kept on the device as `renuabl.design.team-window` and `renuabl.design.team-list-w`; a double-click resets). Sol's design request reads only what happened and what is needed (`designAskText`: "Call with Michelle complete. Design required.", "Tom wrote in.", "Tom's enquiry is in."), the customer's words behind **Why** on the card; older requests are read the same way (`askWords`), in the card and the window's Deals list. Not built yet: files and reactions in a deal's chat, Sol's notes from real events (a proposal opened, a customer gone quiet), routing a request to the right person rather than a label, and requests of other kinds.
- **Deal clock, heat, email opens and attachments (1 Nov 2026, Martin: Primero's copied across; wave 5).** `domain/design/deal-heat.ts`, `email-opens.ts` (tested), `server/design-pulse-repo.ts` (tables `design_email_opens`, `design_deal_files`), `/api/design/pulse`, `/api/o/[token]`, `/api/design/jobs/[id]/opens` and `/files`, `services/deal-pulse.ts`, `components/design/deal-pulse.tsx`; `design-pulse-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`. **On a deal still being sold** (lead, designed, proposal; `hasPulse`): the **deal clock** (`dealClock`: the usual days from new lead to signed sale, `LEAD_DAYS` 21 until a business sets its own, counted in the market's days; green, amber, red by thirds, then over) and the **temperature** (`dealHeat`: hot at 4 and over, cold below 0, else warm, with the reasons, strongest first) read by rule from the business's own record: how lately the customer wrote in their proposal chat, whether they answered us or we are chasing (our chat replies and emails from the deal), the leaning of what they last wrote (plain phrases only), a proposal out and opened, an email opened, a call logged this week, no word yet, and past the clock. A guide, never a forecast. The deal page shows both under the name (`DealPulseBadges`; the temperature opens to say why); board cards show the dot, the clock in words and **Call first 1–3** for the top three not cold (`rankPulses`, `boardPriority`: heat first, then the clock), and the board's columns order deals by it. **Email opens**: every email sent from a deal carries a one-pixel picture at an address of its own (`openPixel`, `/api/o/<32 hex>`, only when `SITE_URL` is set; the address carries nothing but the token), and loading it puts the open against that email (two within a minute are one, up to 20 kept); the deal's Activities say "Opened today at 3:15 pm" / "Not opened yet" under each email (`openedWords`). An open is a mail app loading pictures, so a guide, not proof (Apple Mail opens every email on arrival). **Attachments** (a tab on the deal, `DealFiles`): bills, photos, signed forms kept on the deal (pictures, PDFs, Word, Excel, CSV, text; 10 MB each, 100 a deal; in Postgres), added by the button or dropped on the tab, shown or downloaded only for members who may see the deal, taken off by any of them.
- **The Marketplace, part one: job listings (1 Nov 2026, Martin: "job listings just like Airbnb listings, and then bid to take on the installation"; the network effect).** `domain/design/marketplace.ts` (tested), `server/design-marketplace-repo.ts` (tables `design_listings`, `design_bids`), `/api/design/marketplace` and `/[id]`, `services/marketplace.ts`, `components/design/marketplace.tsx`; `design-marketplace-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`. **Marketplace** is its own section in the top pill: **Job listings** (`/design/marketplace`), **My listings** (`/mine`), **My bids** (`/bids`), a listing at `/design/marketplace/<id>`. **The first thing one business shows others, so the rule is narrower than anywhere else**: a listing row holds only what is chosen to be shown, built on the server from the job (never sent from a browser): the suburb alone (`suburbAlone`), state and postcode, the place rounded to about a kilometre (`roughPlace`, for distance and a suburb-zoom area map only, never the home), the system in words and sizes (kW, panels, panel, inverter, battery kWh, EV charger, storeys, roof, phase, from the deal and its design), the work window, a guide price ex GST or open to offers, the poster's business name; **never the customer's name, phone, email or street, never the poster's costs or margin, and a business's id never leaves the server**. Words on a listing or a bid go through `publicWords` (phone numbers, emails, links and street addresses taken out: contact is exchanged through the marketplace). **Listing** (the deal's **List on the marketplace**, for a sold job not yet installed, `PRE_INSTALL_STAGES`; needs the `publish` permission; one open listing a job; takes bids 3, 7 or 14 days): the box says first what others will and won't see; written on the deal's record. **Browsing**: open listings not your own, as cards the way stays are shown (the area map at suburb zoom, the kW large, battery and EV marks, the suburb, how far from your base (Job map's base), the window, the guide, bids so far, the poster), within 25, 50 or 100 km or anywhere (kept on the device), the soonest closing first (`browse`). **Bidding** (needs `publish`): a price for the installation ex GST, the first day they could start and a short note; one bid a business a listing, changed or taken back while open; never on your own listing, a closed or an awarded one (`bidRefusal`). **The poster alone sees the bids**, each with the business behind it (lowest first, `bidOrder`); a bidder sees only its own and the count. The poster is emailed from RENUABL on a new bid (`newBidEmail`) when email is set up. **Accepting a bid** (the poster, `awardBid`, under a lock): that bid accepted, the rest declined, the listing awarded, the deal's installer set to that business and written on its record, the winner emailed (`awardedEmail`); **only then do the poster and the winner see the street address and each other's business and email** (`handover`); the customer's own details stay with the poster to pass on. The poster can take an open listing down (open bids declined). **Watching and job alerts** (3 Nov 2026, Martin: as eBay and Facebook Marketplace; `domain/design/market-alerts.ts` tested, `server/design-market-watch-repo.ts` (tables `design_watches`, `design_market_alerts`), `/api/design/marketplace/watch`, `/api/design/trade/watch` (one handler, `design-watch-route.ts`), `/api/design/marketplace/alerts`, `market-watch.tsx`, `market-watching.tsx`; `design-market-watch-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`): a person (not the business) watches a job listing still taking bids or an item for sale, never their own business's (the eye over the picture on a card, Watch on the page); everyone sees how many watch (`watchWords`), never who (`withWatches`, added by the routes). **Watching** (`/design/marketplace/watching`, every kind; an inspector sees equipment only) lists both. A watched job closing tomorrow that the watcher's business hasn't bid on is emailed once from the daily cron (`remindWatchers`, `closingSoonEmail`); an item marked sold or taken down emails its watchers and ends their watches (`tellItemWatchers`, `itemGoneEmail`). **Job alerts** (the card on Job listings, `JobAlertsCard`): each person's own, on or off, within 25, 50 or 100 km of the business's base or anywhere, battery jobs only (`cleanAlert`, `alertMatches`: a job of unknown distance reaches only an alert for anywhere); a new listing emails the people it matches in retailers and installers other than the poster, once each, after the answer has gone back (`alertRecipients`, `alertNewListing`, `newListingEmail` with the way to turn them off; at most `MAX_ALERT_EMAILS`). Emails only when email and `SITE_URL` are set up. Not built yet: payment or fees, ratings, messages between the two before awarding, closing listings automatically (a listing past its day simply stops taking bids), and part two, trading spare equipment.
- **The Marketplace, part two: trading spare equipment (1 Nov 2026, Martin: "trade unwanted solar equipment... it would work like facebook marketplace").** `domain/design/trade.ts` (tested), `server/design-trade-repo.ts` (tables `design_trade_items`, `design_trade_photos`, `design_trade_threads`, `design_trade_messages`), `/api/design/trade`, `/[id]`, `/[id]/photos`, `/photos/[photoId]`, `services/trade.ts`, `components/design/trade.tsx`; `design-trade-db.test.ts` runs with `npm run test:db`. Marketplace gains **Equipment** (`/design/marketplace/equipment`) and **My equipment** (`/mine`, with an orange count of conversations with lines unread, `tradeUnread`), **Sell equipment** (`/new`) and an item at `/equipment/<id>`. **Selling** (needs `publish`): the kind (`TRADE_CATEGORIES`), a title, make, model, how many, condition (`CONDITIONS`), a price each ex GST or Make an offer, pick up or delivery, a description, where it is (the address search, kept only as the suburb and a point rounded to about a kilometre, `placeOf`) and up to six photos (each redrawn by `safeImage`, so nothing but the picture is kept: no location from the camera); the title and description go through `publicWords`. The seller marks it Available, Pending or Sold, or takes it down, and adds or removes photos. **Browsing**: items for sale (available, then pending) not your own, as square photo cards with the price, title, how many, suburb and distance from your base; by kind, by every word of a search, within 25, 50 or 100 km or anywhere (`browseItems`). **Messages** (`sendMessage`): a buyer writes to the seller from the item (asking is anyone's); each buyer business has its own conversation (`design_trade_threads`), read only by it and the seller; the seller replies within one (needs `publish`); the other side is emailed from RENUABL (`askedEmail`) only when theirs is the first line they haven't read; opening a conversation reads it. Businesses may share their own contact in messages. A business's id never leaves the server, only its name. A sold or taken-down item disappears from others but stays for a buyer already talking about it; photos are served only for an item the business may see. **RENUABL takes no part in the sale**: no payment, fees or delivery pass through it (the page says so).

the menu inside the page pad, under the main menu): once open, every Marketplace page starts with its own menu at the top of the pad (`MarketplaceMenu` in marketplace-bar.tsx, with `MarketplaceMark`): the mark white in the blue round (Marketplace home), then the kind's pages (`marketplacePagesFor` in sections.ts, tested). While closed the pad shows no menu: the demo carries it. The Marketplace button sits in the middle of the room between the sections and the right-hand card (3 Nov 2026, so the sections centre), its words beside its round only from 1536px. **The launch page's first fold is the Marketplace** (Martin): no coming-soon pill; the headline, one line, then **Sign up** on a row of its own (`Waitlist quick`: an email and a large Sign up, nothing to choose, `LAUNCH.signUp`, `signUpNote`; the foot of the page keeps the fuller form with the I want to choices), then the demo; the network drawing illustrates Why it matters. **The demo** (`MarketplaceShowroom compact` in marketplace-showroom.tsx; copy `LAUNCH.showroom`) fits one fold (the window's height less the top bar, a little wider than the page's column on a wide screen, each view scrolling inside it), headed by the Marketplace's own menu, each page opening its view (`TABS`): **Job listings** as an installer (the feed, `MarketplaceFeed bare`: fifteen made-up jobs, each roof drawn as an aerial photograph by `AerialRoof` in aerial-roof.tsx (hip, gable, L-shaped, skillion or commercial flat; tile or metal; lit and shaded faces, ridges, gutters, shadows; panels laid from the gutter up; everything round the roof blurred; never a real home); a filter change says the count with the filters in words, goes back to the top and fades the results in) or as a retailer (sold deals to list, the listing with its roof, sealed bids arriving lowest first, Accept this bid: sent on RENUABL and connected), **Buy & sell** (kinds, search, drawn stock cards, a message to the seller answered), and **Find partners** (the opt-in directory by kind, Connect, then what connecting allows).
